=== FILE: 4_stm32/src/mt6816_spi.h ===
#ifndef MT6816_SPI_H
#define MT6816_SPI_H

#include "config_pins.h"
#include <Arduino.h>
#include <SPI.h>
#include <SimpleFOC.h>

// ============================================================================
// MT6816 HIGH-PERFORMANCE 14-BIT MAGNETIC ANGLE SENSOR DRIVER
// ============================================================================
// Single-Read Hardware SPI Caching for 20.0 kHz Real-Time SimpleFOC
// Commutation.
// - Timed single-read per tick: loopFOC() touches ZERO SPI bus cycles.
// - Direct Port A/B BSRR atomic register toggling for CS assertion (< 6 ns).
// - 128-point piecewise linear interpolation LUT for sub-0.05 deg linearity.
// ============================================================================

class MT6816Sensor : public Sensor {
public:
  int cs_pin;
  GPIO_TypeDef *cs_port;
  uint32_t cs_set_mask;
  uint32_t cs_reset_mask;

  // Diagnostics & Tracking
  volatile uint32_t total_reads;
  volatile uint32_t parity_errors;
  volatile uint32_t parity_retries;
  volatile uint32_t mag_faults;
  volatile uint32_t consecutive_parity_errors;
  volatile bool is_connected;
  volatile uint16_t slow_poll_counter;
  volatile bool last_no_mag;
  volatile bool last_parity_ok;
  volatile uint8_t last_raw_h;
  volatile uint8_t last_raw_l;
  volatile uint16_t last_raw_14bit;
  uint32_t last_update_tick;
  float zero_offset_deg;
  volatile float cached_calibrated_angle;

  // 128-Point Stepped Piecewise Linear Interpolation LUT
  float lut_errors[128];
  bool is_lut_active;

  SPISettings spi_settings;
  SPIClass *spi_bus;
  int sck_pin;
  int miso_pin;
  int mosi_pin;

  MT6816Sensor(int pin = PIN_M1_CS, SPIClass *bus = &SPI,
               int sck = PIN_SPI1_SCK, int miso = PIN_SPI1_MISO,
               int mosi = PIN_SPI1_MOSI)
      : cs_pin(pin), spi_bus(bus), sck_pin(sck), miso_pin(miso), mosi_pin(mosi),
        cs_port(GPIOB), cs_set_mask(1 << 2), cs_reset_mask((1 << 2) << 16),
        total_reads(0), parity_errors(0), parity_retries(0), mag_faults(0),
        consecutive_parity_errors(0), is_connected(true), slow_poll_counter(0),
        last_no_mag(false), last_parity_ok(true), last_raw_h(0), last_raw_l(0),
        last_raw_14bit(0), last_update_tick(0xFFFFFFFF), zero_offset_deg(0.0f),
        cached_calibrated_angle(0.0f), is_lut_active(false),
        spi_settings(SPI_SPEED_HZ, MSBFIRST, SPI_MODE3) {
    setupCSPin(pin);
    for (int i = 0; i < 128; i++) {
      lut_errors[i] = 0.0f;
    }
  }

  void setSPIBus(SPIClass *bus, int sck, int miso, int mosi) {
    spi_bus = bus;
    sck_pin = sck;
    miso_pin = miso;
    mosi_pin = mosi;
  }

  void setupCSPin(int pin) {
    cs_pin = pin;
    pinMode(cs_pin, OUTPUT);
    digitalWrite(cs_pin, HIGH);

    if (pin == PA15) {
      cs_port = GPIOA;
      cs_set_mask = (1 << 15);
    } else if (pin == PB2) {
      cs_port = GPIOB;
      cs_set_mask = (1 << 2);
    } else if (pin == PB3) {
      cs_port = GPIOB;
      cs_set_mask = (1 << 3);
    } else if (pin == PB11) {
      cs_port = GPIOB;
      cs_set_mask = (1 << 11);
    } else if (pin == PA2) {
      cs_port = GPIOA;
      cs_set_mask = (1 << 2);
    } else if (pin == PA3) {
      cs_port = GPIOA;
      cs_set_mask = (1 << 3);
    } else {
      cs_port = digitalPinToPort(pin);
      cs_set_mask = digitalPinToBitMask(pin);
    }
    cs_reset_mask = cs_set_mask << 16;
  }

  inline void csLow() __attribute__((always_inline)) {
    cs_port->BSRR = cs_reset_mask;
  }

  inline void csHigh() __attribute__((always_inline)) {
    cs_port->BSRR = cs_set_mask;
  }

  static inline void delayNanos(uint32_t ns) __attribute__((always_inline)) {
    // At 170 MHz, 1 cycle = 5.88 ns -> (ns * 17) / 100 cycles
    uint32_t cycles = (ns * 17) / 100;
    uint32_t start = DWT->CYCCNT;
    while ((DWT->CYCCNT - start) < cycles) {
      __NOP();
    }
  }

  static inline bool parityCheck(uint16_t data) {
    // Even parity over 16 bits (count % 2 == 0)
    data ^= data >> 8;
    data ^= data >> 4;
    data ^= data >> 2;
    data ^= data >> 1;
    return (data & 1) == 0;
  }

  void resetErrors() {
    parity_errors = 0;
    parity_retries = 0;
    mag_faults = 0;
    consecutive_parity_errors = 0;
    last_parity_ok = true;
    is_connected = true;
    slow_poll_counter = 0;
  }

  void init() override {
    setupCSPin(cs_pin);
    resetErrors();

    // Configure hardware SPI peripheral
    if (spi_bus != nullptr) {
      spi_bus->setSCLK(sck_pin);
      spi_bus->setMISO(miso_pin);
      spi_bus->setMOSI(mosi_pin);
      spi_bus->begin();

      // Enable pull-up directly on MISO without destroying AF5 configuration
      if (miso_pin == PB4) {
        GPIOB->PUPDR = (GPIOB->PUPDR & ~(3UL << (4 * 2))) | (1UL << (4 * 2));
      } else if (miso_pin == PB14) {
        GPIOB->PUPDR = (GPIOB->PUPDR & ~(3UL << (14 * 2))) | (1UL << (14 * 2));
      }
    }

    // MT6816 initial read
    readHardwareSPI();

    // Force hardware SPI peripheral into 8-bit frame mode (DS = 0x7) with 8-bit
    // RX FIFO threshold (FRXTH = 1) and BR = /16 (10.625 MHz @ 170 MHz APB) so
    // readDualEncodersParallel()'s direct 8-bit DR accesses shift exact 16-bit
    // frames in ~1.8 us per register!
    SPI_TypeDef *spi_inst = (miso_pin == PB4) ? SPI1 : SPI2;
    spi_inst->CR1 &= ~SPI_CR1_SPE;
    spi_inst->CR1 = (spi_inst->CR1 & ~SPI_CR1_BR_Msk) | (3UL << SPI_CR1_BR_Pos);
    spi_inst->CR2 = (spi_inst->CR2 & ~SPI_CR2_DS_Msk) |
                    (7UL << SPI_CR2_DS_Pos) | SPI_CR2_FRXTH;
    spi_inst->CR1 |= SPI_CR1_SPE;

    // Clear any glitch/noise gathered during power-on initialization
    resetErrors();

    // Initialize SimpleFOC Sensor base class
    this->Sensor::init();
    clearVelocityBuffer();
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Hardware SPI Single-Read Engine (Executed strictly ONCE per 50 us tick)
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  inline void readHardwareSPI() __attribute__((always_inline)) {
    if (!spi_bus)
      return;

    // If sensor is disconnected, throttle to 10 Hz (every 2,000 ticks) so it
    // doesn't poll constantly
    if (!is_connected) {
      if (++slow_poll_counter < 2000) {
        return;
      }
      slow_poll_counter = 0;
    }

    spi_bus->beginTransaction(spi_settings);

    total_reads++;

    // 1. Read High Byte (Register 0x03)
    csLow();
    delayNanos(180); // t_CSS setup time (>= 150 ns)
    uint16_t rx_h = spi_bus->transfer16(0x8300);
    delayNanos(180); // t_CSH hold time (>= 150 ns)
    csHigh();

    delayNanos(180); // t_CS_HIGH deassertion hold time (>= 150 ns)

    // 2. Read Low Byte (Register 0x04)
    csLow();
    delayNanos(180); // t_CSS setup time (>= 150 ns)
    uint16_t rx_l = spi_bus->transfer16(0x8400);
    delayNanos(180); // t_CSH hold time (>= 150 ns)
    csHigh();

    parseRawData(rx_h & 0xFF, rx_l & 0xFF);

    spi_bus->endTransaction();

    // Restore 8-bit frame mode (DS = 7, FRXTH = 1) after transfer16()
    SPI_TypeDef *spi_inst = (miso_pin == PB4) ? SPI1 : SPI2;
    spi_inst->CR1 &= ~SPI_CR1_SPE;
    spi_inst->CR1 = (spi_inst->CR1 & ~SPI_CR1_BR_Msk) | (3UL << SPI_CR1_BR_Pos);
    spi_inst->CR2 = (spi_inst->CR2 & ~SPI_CR2_DS_Msk) |
                    (7UL << SPI_CR2_DS_Pos) | SPI_CR2_FRXTH;
    spi_inst->CR1 |= SPI_CR1_SPE;
  }

  inline void parseRawData(uint8_t h, uint8_t l) {
    last_raw_h = h;
    last_raw_l = l;

    uint16_t angle_data = ((uint16_t)h << 8) | l;
    uint16_t raw14 = ((uint16_t)h << 6) | ((l & 0xFC) >> 2);
    bool no_mag = (l & 0x02) != 0;
    bool parity_ok = parityCheck(angle_data);

    last_no_mag = no_mag;

    if (no_mag) {
      mag_faults++;
    }

    if (parity_ok) {
#if defined(INVERT_ENCODER_DIRECTION) && (INVERT_ENCODER_DIRECTION == 1)
      raw14 = (16384 - raw14) & 0x3FFF;
#endif
      last_raw_14bit = raw14;
      consecutive_parity_errors = 0;
      last_parity_ok = true;
      is_connected = true;
    } else {
      parity_errors++;
      consecutive_parity_errors++;
      if (consecutive_parity_errors >= 4) {
        last_parity_ok = false;
        if (consecutive_parity_errors >= 200 && no_mag) {
          is_connected =
              false; // Mark disconnected to avoid floating line noise
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Calibrated Angle Calculation (Sub-microsecond, Zero SPI reads)
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  inline float getRawMechanicalAngle() const {
    return ((float)last_raw_14bit / 16384.0f) * _2PI;
  }

  inline float getCalibratedAngleRad() const {
    float raw_rad = getRawMechanicalAngle();

    if (!is_lut_active) {
      return raw_rad;
    }

    // 128-point Piecewise Linear Interpolation
    int seg = (last_raw_14bit >> 7) & 0x7F; // 0 .. 127
    float frac = (float)(last_raw_14bit & 0x7F) / 128.0f;
    int next_seg = (seg + 1) & 0x7F;

    float err = lut_errors[seg] * (1.0f - frac) + lut_errors[next_seg] * frac;
    float angle = raw_rad - err;

    if (angle < 0.0f)
      angle += _2PI;
    else if (angle >= _2PI)
      angle -= _2PI;

    return angle;
  }

  // Standard SimpleFOC Sensor contract:
  // Called by Sensor::update() and motor.loopFOC(). Returns CACHED reading
  // within the same tick!
  float getSensorAngle() override {
    extern volatile uint32_t foc_tick_count;
    if (last_update_tick == foc_tick_count) {
      return cached_calibrated_angle;
    }
    cached_calibrated_angle = getCalibratedAngleRad();
    return cached_calibrated_angle;
  }

  inline float getFastAngle() const __attribute__((always_inline)) {
    return (float)full_rotations * _2PI + cached_calibrated_angle;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // DWT Velocity Window Tracker (Tunable 0.2 .. 6.0 ms)
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  static constexpr uint8_t VEL_HIST_LEN =
      128; // Power-of-2 ring buffer (up to 6.4 ms @ 20 kHz)
  float hist_angle[VEL_HIST_LEN] = {0};
  uint32_t hist_dwt[VEL_HIST_LEN] = {0};
  uint8_t hist_head = 0;
  uint8_t hist_count = 0;
  uint8_t window_samples = (uint8_t)(DEFAULT_DWT_WINDOW_MS * 20.0f + 0.5f);
  float window_ms = DEFAULT_DWT_WINDOW_MS;

  void setDWTWindowMs(float ms) {
    if (ms < 0.2f)
      ms = 0.2f;
    if (ms > 6.0f)
      ms = 6.0f;
    window_ms = ms;
    uint8_t samples = (uint8_t)(ms * 20.0f + 0.5f);
    if (samples < 2)
      samples = 2;
    if (samples > (VEL_HIST_LEN - 2))
      samples = VEL_HIST_LEN - 2;
    window_samples = samples;
  }

  void clearVelocityBuffer() {
    hist_head = 0;
    hist_count = 0;
    this->velocity = 0.0f;
  }

  void update() override {
    // Prevent duplicate updates within the same 50 us FOC tick (e.g. called in
    // focTimerISR then in loopFOC)
    extern volatile uint32_t foc_tick_count;
    if (last_update_tick == foc_tick_count) {
      return;
    }
    last_update_tick = foc_tick_count;

    // Calculate and cache calibrated angle ONCE per tick (eliminates duplicate
    // 128-pt LUT evaluations)
    cached_calibrated_angle = getCalibratedAngleRad();
    float val = cached_calibrated_angle;
    float d_ang = val - angle_prev;
    if (fabsf(d_ang) > (0.8f * _2PI)) {
      full_rotations += (d_ang > 0.0f) ? -1 : 1;
    }
    angle_prev = val;

    // Push current RAW mechanical angle and DWT timestamp into ring buffer.
    // Using raw 14-bit mechanical angle eliminates 128-point piecewise-linear
    // derivative discontinuities (eliminating audible 2.56 kHz whine) while
    // Sensor::update() retains calibrated LUT angle for FOC commutation!
    uint32_t now = DWT->CYCCNT;
    hist_angle[hist_head] = getRawMechanicalAngle();
    hist_dwt[hist_head] = now;
    hist_head = (hist_head + 1) & (VEL_HIST_LEN - 1);
    if (hist_count < VEL_HIST_LEN) {
      hist_count++;
    }
  }

  float getVelocity() override {
    if (hist_count < 2) {
      return 0.0f;
    }

    uint8_t target_span = window_samples;
    uint8_t span = (hist_count >= target_span) ? target_span : hist_count;
    uint8_t oldest = (hist_head - span) & (VEL_HIST_LEN - 1);
    uint8_t newest = (hist_head - 1) & (VEL_HIST_LEN - 1);

    uint32_t dt_cycles = hist_dwt[newest] - hist_dwt[oldest];
    if (dt_cycles == 0) {
      return this->velocity;
    }

    // Exact cycle-level duration in seconds (170 MHz = 170,000,000 cycles/sec)
    float dt = (float)dt_cycles * (1.0f / 170000000.0f);
    float d_angle = hist_angle[newest] - hist_angle[oldest];

    // Unwrap shortest angular path across [-PI, +PI]
    while (d_angle > (float)_PI)
      d_angle -= (float)_2PI;
    while (d_angle < -(float)_PI)
      d_angle += (float)_2PI;

    this->velocity = d_angle / dt;
    return this->velocity;
  }

  float getSingleTurnDeg() const {
    float deg = ((float)last_raw_14bit / 16384.0f) * 360.0f - zero_offset_deg;
    while (deg < 0.0f)
      deg += 360.0f;
    while (deg >= 360.0f)
      deg -= 360.0f;
    return deg;
  }

  void zeroCurrentPosition() {
    zero_offset_deg = ((float)last_raw_14bit / 16384.0f) * 360.0f;
    this->full_rotations = 0;
    clearVelocityBuffer();
  }

  void setLUTLine(uint8_t line_idx, const float *values, uint8_t count) {
    if (line_idx < 8) {
      int start_idx = line_idx * 16;
      for (uint8_t i = 0; i < count && (start_idx + i) < 128; i++) {
        lut_errors[start_idx + i] = values[i];
      }
      if (line_idx == 7) {
        is_lut_active = true;
      }
    }
  }

  void clearLUT() {
    for (int i = 0; i < 128; i++) {
      lut_errors[i] = 0.0f;
    }
    is_lut_active = false;
  }
};

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Dedicated High-Speed Single-SPI Burst Read (< 1.8 us, Zero Cross-Bus Jitter)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
static inline void readEncoderSingleFast(MT6816Sensor &enc, SPI_TypeDef *spi_inst)
    __attribute__((always_inline));

static inline void readEncoderSingleFast(MT6816Sensor &enc, SPI_TypeDef *spi_inst) {
  while (spi_inst->SR & SPI_SR_RXNE) {
    (void)*(__IO uint8_t *)&spi_inst->DR;
  }

  // 1. Read High Byte (Register 0x03)
  enc.csLow();
  MT6816Sensor::delayNanos(120);

  while (!(spi_inst->SR & SPI_SR_TXE))
    ;
  *(__IO uint8_t *)&spi_inst->DR = 0x83;
  *(__IO uint8_t *)&spi_inst->DR = 0x00;

  uint32_t to = 0;
  while ((spi_inst->SR & SPI_SR_BSY) && ++to < 2000)
    ;

  uint8_t d1 = *(__IO uint8_t *)&spi_inst->DR;
  uint8_t h  = *(__IO uint8_t *)&spi_inst->DR;

  MT6816Sensor::delayNanos(60);
  enc.csHigh();
  MT6816Sensor::delayNanos(150);

  // 2. Read Low Byte (Register 0x04)
  enc.csLow();
  MT6816Sensor::delayNanos(120);

  while (!(spi_inst->SR & SPI_SR_TXE))
    ;
  *(__IO uint8_t *)&spi_inst->DR = 0x84;
  *(__IO uint8_t *)&spi_inst->DR = 0x00;

  to = 0;
  while ((spi_inst->SR & SPI_SR_BSY) && ++to < 2000)
    ;

  uint8_t d2 = *(__IO uint8_t *)&spi_inst->DR;
  uint8_t l  = *(__IO uint8_t *)&spi_inst->DR;

  MT6816Sensor::delayNanos(60);
  enc.csHigh();

  enc.total_reads++;
  enc.parseRawData(h, l);
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Hard Real-Time Parallel Dual-SPI Encoder Read (SPI1 & SPI2 Concurrent Burst)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Simultaneously clocks out 16-bit register reads on SPI1 (Enc1) and SPI2
// (Enc2). Direct STM32G4 hardware FIFO register access (< 3.6 us total for BOTH
// encoders).
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
static inline void readDualEncodersParallel(MT6816Sensor &enc1,
                                            MT6816Sensor &enc2)
    __attribute__((always_inline));

static inline void readDualEncodersParallel(MT6816Sensor &enc1,
                                            MT6816Sensor &enc2) {
  // 1. Drain any residual bytes in RX FIFOs
  while (SPI1->SR & SPI_SR_RXNE) {
    (void)*(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI1->DR;
  }
  while (SPI2->SR & SPI_SR_RXNE) {
    (void)*(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI2->DR;
  }

  // 2. Assert both Chip Selects
  enc1.csLow();
  enc2.csLow();
  MT6816Sensor::delayNanos(120); // t_CSS setup time (>= 100 ns)

  // 3. Simultaneously queue 0x8300 on SPI1 and SPI2 TX FIFOs
  while (!(SPI1->SR & SPI_SR_TXE))
    ;
  *(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI1->DR = 0x83;
  *(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI1->DR = 0x00;

  while (!(SPI2->SR & SPI_SR_TXE))
    ;
  *(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI2->DR = 0x83;
  *(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI2->DR = 0x00;

  // Both SPI1 and SPI2 hardware engines are now shifting 16 bits in parallel!

  // 4. Wait for both SPI1 and SPI2 engines to finish shifting in parallel
  uint32_t to = 0;
  while (((SPI1->SR & SPI_SR_BSY) || (SPI2->SR & SPI_SR_BSY)) && ++to < 2000)
    ;

  uint8_t d1 = *(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI1->DR;
  uint8_t h1 = *(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI1->DR;
  uint8_t d2 = *(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI2->DR;
  uint8_t h2 = *(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI2->DR;

  // 5. Deassert both CS lines
  MT6816Sensor::delayNanos(60);
  enc1.csHigh();
  enc2.csHigh();

  MT6816Sensor::delayNanos(150); // t_CS_HIGH hold time (>= 150 ns)

  // --- Register 0x04 (Low Byte + Parity + Status) ---

  // 6. Assert both Chip Selects
  enc1.csLow();
  enc2.csLow();
  MT6816Sensor::delayNanos(120);

  // 7. Simultaneously queue 0x8400 on SPI1 and SPI2 TX FIFOs
  while (!(SPI1->SR & SPI_SR_TXE))
    ;
  *(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI1->DR = 0x84;
  *(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI1->DR = 0x00;

  while (!(SPI2->SR & SPI_SR_TXE))
    ;
  *(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI2->DR = 0x84;
  *(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI2->DR = 0x00;

  // 8. Wait for both SPI1 and SPI2 engines to finish shifting in parallel
  to = 0;
  while (((SPI1->SR & SPI_SR_BSY) || (SPI2->SR & SPI_SR_BSY)) && ++to < 2000)
    ;

  uint8_t d3 = *(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI1->DR;
  uint8_t l1 = *(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI1->DR;
  uint8_t d4 = *(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI2->DR;
  uint8_t l2 = *(__IO uint8_t *)&SPI2->DR;

  // 9. Deassert both CS lines
  MT6816Sensor::delayNanos(60);
  enc1.csHigh();
  enc2.csHigh();

  // 12. Parse angles and parity
  enc1.total_reads++;
  enc2.total_reads++;
  enc1.parseRawData(h1, l1);
  enc2.parseRawData(h2, l2);
}

#endif // MT6816_SPI_H


=== FILE: 5_webots/README.md ===
# Webots Simulation & Control Architecture

This directory contains the Webots physics simulation environments, robot models, autonomous controllers, C++ Curvilinear SL-MPC trajectory optimizers, genetic algorithm optimization pipelines, and validation test benches for the Folkrace vehicle.

---

## 1. Coordinate Systems & Sign Conventions

All Webots controllers, MPC algorithms, sensor fusion models, and recovery managers adhere strictly to the following conventions:

### A. Robot Body Frame (Vehicle Local Frame)
* **+Y Axis**: **Forward** (Longitudinal vehicle motion)
* **-Y Axis**: **Backward** (Reverse motion)
* **+X Axis**: **Right** (Lateral displacement to the right of the vehicle)
* **-X Axis**: **Left** (Lateral displacement to the left of the vehicle)
* **+Z Axis**: **Up** (Normal to track surface)

### B. Steering Angle Convention (`cmd_steer_rad`, `steer_deg`)
* **Positive (+)**: **Steer LEFT** (Counter-Clockwise / CCW yaw turn)
* **Negative (-)**: **Steer RIGHT** (Clockwise / CW yaw turn)

### C. Yaw & Angular Rates (`yaw`, `gyro_z`, `theta_g`)
* **Positive (+)**: **Turning Left** (CCW)
* **Negative (-)**: **Turning Right** (CW)

---

## 2. Simulation Architecture & Controller Suite

The Webots simulation stack models the distributed physical architecture (Raspberry Pi 4 brain, ESP32 dynamics, and quad ToF perception hub):

* **`simulation/my_project/controllers/tof_path_planner/`**:
  * **Curvilinear SL-MPC (`planner_sl_mpc.py` & `cpp/curvilinear_sl_mpc.cpp`)**: High-performance Frenet-frame trajectory planner formulated as a Quadratic Program (QP) and solved via OSQP in compiled C++ (`curvilinear_sl_mpc.dll`). Matches the deployment on the Raspberry Pi 4.
  * **Quad ToF Perception Model (`cpp_smart_hybrid_costmap.py`)**: Simulates the 4x VL53L8CX multizone ToF array (16 distance readings), performing closest-obstacle extraction and dynamic costmap updates.
  * **4WD Electronic Differential (`electronic_diff_4wd.py`)**: Replicates the ESP32 Main dynamics engine, computing individual wheel velocities for all 4 direct-drive motors under true Ackermann geometry.
  * **Anti-Wrong-Way Watchdog (`anti_wrong_way_watchdog.py`)**: Monitors track heading progress and commands immediate 180-degree recovery spins if inverted.
  * **Stuck Recovery Manager (`stuck_recovery_manager.py`)**: Multi-stage escape state machine executing reverse maneuvers and clearance scans if the vehicle stalls.
* **`optimization/ga_planner_optimizer/`**:
  * Multi-core headless Genetic Algorithm tuning pipeline optimizing MPC weights, lookahead horizons, and speed profiles.
* **`worlds/`**:
  * Active Webots simulation worlds (e.g. `track_racing.wbt` with modular 1200mm track walls and dynamic obstacles).

=== FILE: 5_webots/optimization/ga_planner_optimizer/README.md ===
# Headless Genetic Algorithm (GA) Optimizer for Autonomous ToF Racing

A dedicated high-throughput multi-core Genetic Algorithm framework designed specifically for optimizing autonomous ToF path planners in Webots without MPPI.

---

## 1. Quick Start

### Launch GA Optimization (1-Click)
```bash
# Optimize Option 1 (BVP Dynamic State Lattice)
python launch_ga_optimization.py --planner option1

# Optimize Option 2 (Curvilinear Frenet SL-MPC)
python launch_ga_optimization.py --planner option2

# Optimize Option 3 (7th-Order Septic Spline) with custom cores and generations
python launch_ga_optimization.py --planner option3 --cores 6 --generations 40 --pop-size 24
```

### Resume an Interrupted Run
```bash
python launch_ga_optimization.py --planner option1 --resume
```

### Plot Convergence Progress
```bash
python plot_ga_results.py
```

---

## 2. Configuration & Parameter Tuning: `ga_planner_config.py`

All GA hyperparameters, worker allocations, evaluation horizons, and path planner gene tables are centralized in [ga_planner_config.py](file:///c:/Users/nurmi/Documents/Folkrace/5_5_webots/optimization/ga_planner_optimizer/ga_planner_config.py).

### Core GA Hyperparameters
- `NUM_CPU_CORES`: Number of parallel Webots instances running across CPU cores (default: auto-detect physical/logical cores).
- `POPULATION_SIZE`: Number of candidate chromosomes evaluated per generation (default: 24).
- `NUM_GENERATIONS`: Maximum optimization generations (default: 30).
- `EVALUATION_DURATION_S`: Simulation evaluation duration per candidate (strictly 20.0 seconds).
- `ACTIVE_PLANNER`: `"OPTION_1_BVP"`, `"OPTION_2_SL_MPC"`, or `"OPTION_3_SEPTIC"`.
- `CROSSOVER_RATE`: Arithmetic blend crossover probability (default: 0.80).
- `MUTATION_RATE`: Gene mutation probability (default: 0.18).
- `ELITISM_COUNT`: Number of top elite candidates preserved untouched (default: 2).

### Moving Obstacle Reverse Motion
- `ENABLE_MOVING_OBSTACLES = True`
- `MOVING_OBSTACLE_SPEED_MPS = 0.50`
- Obstacles travel **against the robot heading** along the track racing line (`cyan_champion_trajectory.csv`) at 0.50 m/s. The racing line is used solely for moving obstacles, not for path planners.

### 20-Second Distance Fitness Function
- Fitness = `(distance_m * 100.0) + (mean_speed_mps * 25.0) + (laps_completed * 1500.0)`
- Hard Penalty on Crash: `-5000.0`
- Hard Penalty on Stall (`v < 0.15 m/s` for `> 1.5 s`): `-3000.0`

---

## 3. Directory Layout

```
5_webots/optimization/ga_planner_optimizer/
├── ga_planner_config.py         # All exposed GA parameters and gene bounds
├── ga_optimizer.py              # Parallel GA engine (tournament selection, elitism, checkpointing)
├── ga_evaluator.py              # Headless Webots worker pool manager
├── test_ga_optimizer_offline.py # Offline verification tests
└── data/
    └── cyan_champion_trajectory.csv # Racing line for moving obstacles
```

---

## 4. Output Artifacts & Checkpoints
- `best_planner_genome.json`: JSON file containing the highest-fitness champion chromosome and metrics.
- `ga_checkpoint.json`: Checkpoint saved after each generation to allow resuming anytime.
- `ga_history.csv`: Generational progression table (Best Fitness, Distance, Velocity, Crashes).
- `ga_convergence_summary.png`: Visual plots generated by `plot_ga_results.py`.

=== FILE: 5_webots/optimization/ga_planner_optimizer/ga_evaluator.py ===
"""
================================================================================
  HEADLESS WEBOTS EVALUATOR & PARALLEL WORKER POOL
================================================================================
  Spawns and manages long-lived headless Webots processes across CPU cores.
  Communicates with supervisor workers via local TCP sockets for sub-second trial
  dispatch, collecting 20-second distance metrics and scalar fitness.
================================================================================
"""

import os
import sys
import json
import time
import socket
import subprocess
import queue
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Any
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
import numpy as np

CURR_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

def _find_world_path(start_dir: str) -> str:
    p = start_dir
    for _ in range(6):
        cand = os.path.join(p, "5_webots", "simulation", "my_project", "worlds", "track_racing.wbt")
        if os.path.exists(cand):
            return cand
        cand2 = os.path.join(p, "simulation", "my_project", "worlds", "track_racing.wbt")
        if os.path.exists(cand2):
            return cand2
        parent = os.path.dirname(p)
        if parent == p:
            break
        p = parent
    return os.path.join(start_dir, "track_racing.wbt")

DEFAULT_WORLD_PATH = _find_world_path(CURR_DIR)
OPTIMIZER_PROJECT_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(DEFAULT_WORLD_PATH)) if os.path.exists(DEFAULT_WORLD_PATH) else CURR_DIR
MY_PROJECT_DIR = OPTIMIZER_PROJECT_DIR
WEBOTS_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(MY_PROJECT_DIR))
WORKSPACE_ROOT = os.path.dirname(WEBOTS_DIR)

for p in [OPTIMIZER_PROJECT_DIR, CURR_DIR]:
    if p not in sys.path and os.path.exists(p):
        sys.path.insert(0, p)

from ga_planner_config import (
    NUM_CPU_CORES,
    WEBOTS_BINARY,
    BASE_WORKER_PORT,
    POPULATION_SIZE,
    NUM_GENERATIONS,
    EVALUATION_DURATION_S,
    TIMEOUT_PER_TRIAL_WALL_S,
    ACTIVE_PLANNER,
    compute_scalar_fitness,
    get_genome_class,
    BasePlannerGenome,
    USE_TRACK2,
)


class HeadlessWebotsWorker:
    """Manages a single long-lived headless Webots process on a dedicated port."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        worker_id: int,
        base_port: int = BASE_WORKER_PORT,
        webots_bin: str = WEBOTS_BINARY,
        world_path: str = DEFAULT_WORLD_PATH,
        rendered: bool = False,
        controller: Optional[any] = None,
    ):
        self.worker_id = worker_id
        self.port = base_port + worker_id
        self.webots_bin = webots_bin
        self.world_path = world_path
        self.rendered = rendered
        self.controller = controller
        self.proc: Optional[subprocess.Popen] = None
        self.log_file = None
        self._is_stopped = False
        self.start_process()

    def _kill_proc(self):
        """Kills active process tree and closes log file without marking worker permanently stopped."""
        if self.proc is not None:
            pid = self.proc.pid
            # Try graceful TCP shutdown first
            try:
                s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
                s.settimeout(0.5)
                s.connect(("127.0.0.1", self.port))
                s.sendall((json.dumps({"cmd": "SHUTDOWN"}) + "\n").encode("utf-8"))
                s.close()
            except Exception:
                pass

            # Force-kill the entire process tree
            if sys.platform == "win32":
                try:
                    subprocess.run(
                        ["taskkill", "/F", "/T", "/PID", str(pid)],
                        capture_output=True,
                        timeout=5,
                    )
                except Exception:
                    pass
            else:
                try:
                    import signal as _signal
                    os.killpg(os.getpgid(pid), _signal.SIGKILL)
                except Exception:
                    pass

            try:
                self.proc.wait(timeout=2.0)
            except Exception:
                try:
                    self.proc.kill()
                except Exception:
                    pass
            self.proc = None

        if self.log_file is not None and not self.log_file.closed:
            try:
                self.log_file.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
            self.log_file = None

    def start_process(self):
        """Launches Webots in fast headless batch mode or rendered real-time preview mode."""
        if self._is_stopped:
            return
        if self.controller and (getattr(self.controller, "abort_requested", False) or getattr(self.controller, "stop_requested", False)):
            self._is_stopped = True
            return

        self._kill_proc()

        env = os.environ.copy()
        webots_bin_dir = os.path.dirname(self.webots_bin)
        webots_home = r"C:\Program Files\Webots"
        py_dir = os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
        py_scripts = os.path.join(py_dir, "Scripts")
        env["WEBOTS_HOME"] = webots_home
        env["PATH"] = webots_bin_dir + os.pathsep + py_dir + os.pathsep + py_scripts + os.pathsep + env.get("PATH", "")
        env["PYTHONPATH"] = os.pathsep.join([
            CURR_DIR,
            OPTIMIZER_PROJECT_DIR,
            os.path.join(OPTIMIZER_PROJECT_DIR, "controllers", "tof_path_planner"),
            os.path.join(CURR_DIR, "data"),
            WORKSPACE_ROOT,
        ])
        env["GA_WORKER_PORT"] = str(self.port)
        env["GA_WORKER_ID"] = str(self.worker_id)
        env["WEBOTS_CONTROLLER_MODE"] = "ga_eval"
        env["PYTHONUNBUFFERED"] = "1"

        if self.rendered:
            # Rendered real-time visual mode for human inspection
            cmd = [
                self.webots_bin,
                f"--port={self.port}",
                "--mode=realtime",
                "--batch",
                self.world_path,
            ]
            startupinfo = None
            creationflags = 0
        else:
            # Headless multi-core fast batch mode
            cmd = [
                self.webots_bin,
                f"--port={self.port}",
                "--mode=fast",
                "--no-rendering",
                "--batch",
                "--minimize",
                "--stdout",
                "--stderr",
                self.world_path,
            ]
            startupinfo = None
            creationflags = 0
            if sys.platform == "win32":
                startupinfo = subprocess.STARTUPINFO()
                startupinfo.dwFlags |= subprocess.STARTF_USESHOWWINDOW
                startupinfo.wShowWindow = 0  # SW_HIDE
                creationflags = getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0x08000000)

        if self._is_stopped or (self.controller and getattr(self.controller, "abort_requested", False)):
            return

        try:
            log_dir = os.path.join(CURR_DIR, "logs")
            os.makedirs(log_dir, exist_ok=True)
            self.log_path = os.path.join(log_dir, f"worker_{self.worker_id}.log")
            self.log_file = open(self.log_path, "w", encoding="utf-8")

            self.proc = subprocess.Popen(
                cmd,
                env=env,
                cwd=OPTIMIZER_PROJECT_DIR,
                startupinfo=startupinfo,
                creationflags=creationflags,
                stdout=self.log_file,
                stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            )
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[Worker {self.worker_id}] Error launching Webots executable: {e}", flush=True)
            return

        # Poll socket until worker is up (up to 45 seconds)
        t0 = time.time()
        connected = False
        while time.time() - t0 < 45.0:
            if self._is_stopped or (self.controller and getattr(self.controller, "abort_requested", False)):
                self.stop_process()
                return
            if self.ping():
                connected = True
                break
            time.sleep(0.3)

        if not connected and not self._is_stopped:
            print(f"[Worker {self.worker_id}] Warning: Timed out connecting to port {self.port}.", flush=True)
        elif connected:
            print(f"  [Worker {self.worker_id}] Connected and ready on Port {self.port}.", flush=True)

    def ping(self) -> bool:
        """Checks if Webots supervisor worker server is listening."""
        try:
            s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
            s.settimeout(1.0)
            s.connect(("127.0.0.1", self.port))
            req = json.dumps({"cmd": "PING"}) + "\n"
            s.sendall(req.encode("utf-8"))
            data = s.recv(1024)
            s.close()
            return b"PONG" in data
        except Exception:
            return False

    def evaluate(
        self,
        planner_name: str,
        gene_dict: Dict[str, Any],
        duration_s: float = EVALUATION_DURATION_S,
        use_track2: bool = USE_TRACK2,
    ) -> Tuple[float, Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Sends candidate genome to Webots supervisor and receives 20s run metrics."""
        fallback_metrics = {
            "distance_m": 0.0,
            "mean_speed_mps": 0.0,
            "laps_completed": 0,
            "crashed": True,
            "stalled": False,
            "aborted": True,
            "fitness": -5000.0,
        }

        if self._is_stopped or (self.controller and getattr(self.controller, "abort_requested", False)):
            return -5000.0, fallback_metrics

        for attempt in range(2):
            if self._is_stopped or (self.controller and getattr(self.controller, "abort_requested", False)):
                return -5000.0, fallback_metrics

            try:
                s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
                s.settimeout(TIMEOUT_PER_TRIAL_WALL_S)
                s.connect(("127.0.0.1", self.port))

                req = json.dumps({
                    "cmd": "EVAL",
                    "planner": planner_name,
                    "genes": gene_dict,
                    "duration_s": duration_s,
                    "use_track2": use_track2,
                }) + "\n"

                s.sendall(req.encode("utf-8"))

                # Read response until newline
                buf = ""
                while "\n" not in buf:
                    if self._is_stopped or (self.controller and getattr(self.controller, "abort_requested", False)):
                        try:
                            s.close()
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                        return -5000.0, fallback_metrics
                    chunk = s.recv(4096).decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
                    if not chunk:
                        break
                    buf += chunk
                s.close()

                resp = json.loads(buf.strip())
                if resp.get("status") == "OK":
                    metrics = resp.get("metrics", {})
                    # Worker computes composite fitness (min across Track 1 and Track 2)
                    if "fitness" in metrics:
                        fitness = float(metrics["fitness"])
                    else:
                        fitness = compute_scalar_fitness(metrics, gene_dict=gene_dict, eval_duration_s=duration_s)
                        metrics["fitness"] = fitness
                    return fitness, metrics
                else:
                    err_msg = resp.get("error", "Unknown error from Webots worker")
                    print(f"[Worker {self.worker_id}] Webots server error response: {err_msg}", flush=True)
                    time.sleep(0.5)
                    if not self._is_stopped and not (self.controller and getattr(self.controller, "abort_requested", False)):
                        self.start_process()
            except Exception as e:
                if self._is_stopped or (self.controller and getattr(self.controller, "abort_requested", False)):
                    break
                print(f"[Worker {self.worker_id}] Attempt {attempt + 1} communication failure: {e}", flush=True)
                time.sleep(1.0)
                if not self._is_stopped and not (self.controller and getattr(self.controller, "abort_requested", False)):
                    self.start_process()

        return -5000.0, fallback_metrics

    def stop_process(self):
        """Terminates Webots process, kills child processes, and permanently marks as stopped."""
        self._is_stopped = True
        self._kill_proc()


class ParallelWorkerPool:
    """Manages pool of headless Webots workers for multi-core parallel GA evaluations."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        num_workers: int = NUM_CPU_CORES,
        base_port: int = BASE_WORKER_PORT,
        webots_bin: str = WEBOTS_BINARY,
        world_path: str = DEFAULT_WORLD_PATH,
        controller: Optional[any] = None,
    ):
        self.num_workers = num_workers
        self.controller = controller
        self.workers: List[HeadlessWebotsWorker] = []
        self._shutdown_called = False

        # Wire pool into controller immediately so abort() can shut down workers even during init
        if self.controller is not None:
            self.controller._pool = self

        if self.controller and getattr(self.controller, "abort_requested", False):
            self._shutdown_called = True
            return

        print("=" * 80, flush=True)
        print(f"  INITIALIZING PARALLEL GA WORKER POOL ({num_workers} Webots Instances in Parallel)", flush=True)
        print("=" * 80, flush=True)

        def _init_worker(w_idx: int) -> Optional[HeadlessWebotsWorker]:
            if self._shutdown_called or (self.controller and getattr(self.controller, "abort_requested", False)):
                return None
            w = HeadlessWebotsWorker(
                worker_id=w_idx,
                base_port=base_port,
                webots_bin=webots_bin,
                world_path=world_path,
                controller=self.controller,
            )
            if self._shutdown_called or (self.controller and getattr(self.controller, "abort_requested", False)):
                w.stop_process()
                return None
            return w

        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:
            futures = [executor.submit(_init_worker, i) for i in range(num_workers)]
            for f in futures:
                if self._shutdown_called or (self.controller and getattr(self.controller, "abort_requested", False)):
                    break
                try:
                    w = f.result()
                    if w is not None:
                        self.workers.append(w)
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"[WorkerPool Init Error] {e}", flush=True)

        if self._shutdown_called or (self.controller and getattr(self.controller, "abort_requested", False)):
            self.shutdown()

    def evaluate_population(
        self,
        planner_name: str,
        population_genomes: List[BasePlannerGenome],
        duration_s: float = EVALUATION_DURATION_S,
        controller: Optional[any] = None,
        use_track2: bool = USE_TRACK2,
    ) -> List[Tuple[float, Dict[str, Any]]]:
        """Evaluates an entire generation of candidate genomes in parallel."""
        ctrl = controller or self.controller
        if ctrl and getattr(ctrl, "abort_requested", False):
            return []
        if self._shutdown_called or not self.workers:
            return []

        results: List[Optional[Tuple[float, Dict[str, Any]]]] = [None] * len(population_genomes)

        worker_q = queue.Queue()
        for w in self.workers:
            worker_q.put(w)

        def eval_task(cand_idx: int, genome: BasePlannerGenome):
            if ctrl and getattr(ctrl, "abort_requested", False):
                return cand_idx, -5000.0, {"crashed": True, "aborted": True}
            if self._shutdown_called or not self.workers:
                return cand_idx, -5000.0, {"crashed": True, "aborted": True}
            worker = worker_q.get()
            try:
                fitness, metrics = worker.evaluate(planner_name, genome.to_dict(), duration_s, use_track2=use_track2)
                return cand_idx, fitness, metrics
            finally:
                worker_q.put(worker)

        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=len(self.workers)) as executor:
            futures = []
            for idx, genome in enumerate(population_genomes):
                if ctrl and getattr(ctrl, "abort_requested", False):
                    break
                futures.append(executor.submit(eval_task, idx, genome))

            for future in as_completed(futures):
                if ctrl and getattr(ctrl, "abort_requested", False):
                    for f in futures:
                        f.cancel()
                    break
                try:
                    cand_idx, fitness, metrics = future.result()
                    results[cand_idx] = (fitness, metrics)
                except Exception:
                    pass

        return [r for r in results if r is not None]

    def health_check(self) -> int:
        """Pings all workers in the pool; restarts any unresponsive workers. Returns healthy worker count."""
        if self._shutdown_called or not self.workers:
            return 0
        healthy = 0
        for w in self.workers:
            if w and w.ping():
                healthy += 1
            elif w and not self._shutdown_called:
                print(f"[WorkerPool HealthCheck] Worker {w.worker_id} (Port {w.port}) unresponsive. Auto-restarting...", flush=True)
                try:
                    w.start_process()
                    if w.ping():
                        healthy += 1
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"[WorkerPool HealthCheck] Failed to restart Worker {w.worker_id}: {e}", flush=True)
        return healthy

    def shutdown(self):
        """Stops all Webots worker processes."""
        self._shutdown_called = True
        print("[ParallelWorkerPool] Shutting down all Webots instances...", flush=True)
        for w in list(self.workers):
            if w is not None:
                try:
                    w.stop_process()
                except Exception:
                    pass
        self.workers.clear()


def run_rendered_preview(
    planner_name: str,
    gene_dict: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    duration_s: float = 20.0,
    port: int = 10299,
    controller: Optional[any] = None,
    use_track2: bool = False,
) -> Tuple[float, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Executes a single rendered, real-time 20-second run of the specified planner in Webots GUI.
    Allows user to observe vehicle behavior and abort if anything is wrong.
    """
    if gene_dict is None:
        genome_cls = get_genome_class(planner_name)
        gene_dict = genome_cls().to_dict()

    if controller and (getattr(controller, "abort_requested", False) or getattr(controller, "stop_requested", False)):
        return -5000.0, {"crashed": True, "aborted": True}

    print(f"\n[Preview] Starting 20s Rendered Real-Time Preview for {planner_name} on Port {port}...", flush=True)
    worker = HeadlessWebotsWorker(
        worker_id=99,
        base_port=port,
        rendered=True,
        controller=controller,
    )

    if controller is not None:
        controller._preview_worker = worker

    try:
        if controller and (getattr(controller, "abort_requested", False) or getattr(controller, "stop_requested", False)):
            return -5000.0, {"crashed": True, "aborted": True}

        fitness, metrics = worker.evaluate(
            planner_name=planner_name,
            gene_dict=gene_dict,
            duration_s=duration_s,
            use_track2=use_track2,
        )
        return fitness, metrics
    finally:
        if controller is not None:
            controller._preview_worker = None
        worker.stop_process()

"""
================================================================================
  HIGH-THROUGHPUT PARALLEL GENETIC ALGORITHM (GA) OPTIMIZER
================================================================================
  Optimizes autonomous ToF path planner parameters in headless Webots.
  Features:
    - Multi-core parallel simulation across CPU cores (20s duration)
    - Moving obstacles traveling against robot heading at 0.50 m/s
    - Tournament selection, arithmetic crossover, Gaussian mutation, elitism
    - Real-time checkpointing (ga_checkpoint.json, ga_history.csv, best_genome.json)
================================================================================
"""

import os
import sys
import time
import json
import csv
import signal
import pickle
import argparse
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional, Any
import numpy as np

try:
    import cma
except ImportError:
    cma = None

CURR_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

def _find_optimizer_dir(start_dir: str) -> str:
    if os.path.exists(os.path.join(start_dir, "worlds", "Cyan_Champion_GA.wbt")):
        return start_dir
    p = start_dir
    for _ in range(6):
        cand = os.path.join(p, "5_webots", "optimization", "ga_planner_optimizer")
        if os.path.exists(os.path.join(cand, "worlds", "Cyan_Champion_GA.wbt")):
            return cand
        cand2 = os.path.join(p, "optimization", "ga_planner_optimizer")
        if os.path.exists(os.path.join(cand2, "worlds", "Cyan_Champion_GA.wbt")):
            return cand2
        parent = os.path.dirname(p)
        if parent == p:
            break
        p = parent
    return start_dir

OPTIMIZER_PROJECT_DIR = CURR_DIR
GA_OPTIMIZER_DIR = OPTIMIZER_PROJECT_DIR

]:

        sys.path.insert(0, p)
