            "gap_latch_hysteresis": 1.25, "gap_latch_dist_m": 0.28,
            "alt_min_separation_m": 0.15, "alt_aperture_bias": 1.80, "alt_switch_hysteresis": 8.00,
            "w_gap_clear": 5.00,
            "aggr_dist_far_m": 2.30, "aggr_dist_close_m": 1.00, "aggr_min_scale": 0.72,
            "steer_deadband_rad": 0.002,
            "recovery_clearance_exit_m": 0.25, "recovery_steer_angle_rad": 0.5623,
            "recovery_arc_lookahead_m": 0.13,
        },
        # 1. High-Momentum Apex Striker
        {
            "v_cruise_max": 3.84, "v_cruise_min": 1.50,
            "horizon_N": 30, "dt": 0.016667,
            "mu_friction": 0.80, "r_inflation_m": 0.075,
            "q_progress": 18.0, "q_cte": 14.0, "q_heading": 6.0,
            "r_steer": 3.5, "r_accel": 1.5, "r_steer_rate": 8.0,
            "v_corner_factor": 0.95,
            "center_bias": 0.0,
            "target_lookahead_m": 2.30, "w_gap_distance": 4.00, "w_gap_width": 1.80,
            "w_gap_steer": 0.80, "w_gap_straight": 0.50,
            "w_side_clearance": 2.50,
            "gap_dist_exp_k": 0.70, "gap_dist_thresh_m": 0.95,
            "gap_latch_hysteresis": 1.10, "gap_latch_dist_m": 0.25,
            "alt_min_separation_m": 0.15, "alt_aperture_bias": 1.80, "alt_switch_hysteresis": 7.00,
            "w_gap_clear": 6.00,
            "aggr_dist_far_m": 2.30, "aggr_dist_close_m": 1.10, "aggr_min_scale": 0.75,
            "steer_deadband_rad": 0.002,
            "recovery_clearance_exit_m": 0.25, "recovery_steer_angle_rad": 0.5623,
            "recovery_arc_lookahead_m": 0.12,
        },
        # 2. Safe High-Clearance Sentinel
        {
            "v_cruise_max": 3.84, "v_cruise_min": 1.15,
            "horizon_N": 30, "dt": 0.016667,
            "mu_friction": 0.80, "r_inflation_m": 0.075,
            "q_progress": 10.0, "q_cte": 22.0, "q_heading": 10.0,
            "r_steer": 6.0, "r_accel": 2.5, "r_steer_rate": 12.0,
            "v_corner_factor": 0.90,
            "center_bias": 0.40,
            "target_lookahead_m": 2.30, "w_gap_distance": 3.00, "w_gap_width": 2.20,
            "w_gap_steer": 1.10, "w_gap_straight": 0.40,
            "w_side_clearance": 3.20,
            "gap_dist_exp_k": 0.50, "gap_dist_thresh_m": 0.98,
            "gap_latch_hysteresis": 1.50, "gap_latch_dist_m": 0.32,
            "alt_min_separation_m": 0.20, "alt_aperture_bias": 2.00, "alt_switch_hysteresis": 12.00,
            "w_gap_clear": 4.00,
            "aggr_dist_far_m": 2.30, "aggr_dist_close_m": 1.20, "aggr_min_scale": 0.80,
            "steer_deadband_rad": 0.003,
            "recovery_clearance_exit_m": 0.25, "recovery_steer_angle_rad": 0.5623,
            "recovery_arc_lookahead_m": 0.15,
        },
    ]

    # Inject seed genome as primary archetype if provided
    if seed_genome is not None:
        if isinstance(seed_genome, BasePlannerGenome):
            archetypes[0] = seed_genome.to_dict()
        elif isinstance(seed_genome, dict):
            genes_dict = seed_genome.get("genes") or seed_genome.get("best_genome") or seed_genome
            arch = dict(genes_dict)
            if "alt_lookahead_depth_weight" in arch and "w_gap_clear" not in arch:
                arch["w_gap_clear"] = arch["alt_lookahead_depth_weight"]
            archetypes[0] = arch

    population: List[BasePlannerGenome] = []
    for arch in archetypes:
        if len(population) < pop_size:
            population.append(genome_cls(arch))

    n_remain = pop_size - len(population)
    if n_remain > 0:
        base_arr = genome_cls(archetypes[0]).to_array()
        for k in range(n_remain):
            grid_frac = (k + 0.5) / n_remain
            noise = (np.random.uniform(-0.35, 0.35, size=base_arr.shape) + (grid_frac - 0.5) * 0.50) * span
            cand_arr = np.clip(base_arr + noise, lower, upper)
            cand = genome_cls()
            cand.from_array(cand_arr)
            if "v_cruise_min" in cand.values and "v_cruise_max" in cand.values:
                cand.values["v_cruise_min"] = min(cand.values["v_cruise_min"], cand.values["v_cruise_max"] - 0.30)
            cand.clamp_and_repair()
            population.append(cand)

    return population


def compute_efficiency_multiplier(gene_dict: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> float:
    """SL-MPC uses constant-time native C++ OSQP solves; multiplier is normalized."""
    return 1.0


SPEED_PHYSICAL_MAX_MPS: float = 3.84
DISTANCE_VALID_MIN_M: float = 0.10


def compute_scalar_fitness(
    metrics: Dict[str, Any],
    gene_dict: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    eval_duration_s: Optional[float] = None,
) -> float:
    """Computes duration-normalized scalar optimization fitness from Webots trial metrics.

    Normalizes distance and lap metrics relative to FITNESS_BASE_DURATION_S (25.0s) so that
    longer simulation runs (e.g. 50s-60s) do not inflate the fitness score. When a candidate
    survives longer runs without crashing or stalling, a modest bonus (up to +10% at
    EVAL_DURATION_MAX_S) is applied to reward extended collision-free survival.
    """
    dist_m = float(metrics.get("distance_m", 0.0))
    mean_speed = float(metrics.get("mean_speed_mps", 0.0))
    laps = int(metrics.get("laps_completed", 0))
    crashed = bool(metrics.get("crashed", False))
    stuck = bool(metrics.get("stalled", False))

    if math.isnan(dist_m) or dist_m <= DISTANCE_VALID_MIN_M:
        return -5000.0

    if math.isnan(mean_speed) or mean_speed < 0.0:
        mean_speed = 0.0
    elif mean_speed > SPEED_PHYSICAL_MAX_MPS:
        mean_speed = SPEED_PHYSICAL_MAX_MPS

    # Determine planned trial evaluation duration for normalization
    trial_dur = eval_duration_s
    if trial_dur is None:
        trial_dur = metrics.get("eval_duration_s") or metrics.get("target_duration_s")
    if trial_dur is None:
        # Fallback to duration_s; if crashed/stuck early, guard against small sim_time inflating distance
        raw_dur = float(metrics.get("duration_s", FITNESS_BASE_DURATION_S))
        trial_dur = max(FITNESS_BASE_DURATION_S, raw_dur) if (crashed or stuck) else raw_dur
    trial_dur = max(1.0, float(trial_dur))

    # Duration normalization: scale distance and laps to baseline 25.0s equivalent
    norm_scale = FITNESS_BASE_DURATION_S / trial_dur
    effective_dist_m = dist_m * norm_scale
    effective_laps = laps * norm_scale

    base_score = (
        (effective_dist_m * FITNESS_PROGRESS_WEIGHT)
        + (mean_speed * FITNESS_SPEED_WEIGHT)
        + (effective_laps * FITNESS_LAP_BONUS)
    )

    if crashed:
        return float(base_score - FITNESS_COLLISION_PENALTY)
    elif stuck:
        return float(base_score - FITNESS_STALL_PENALTY)
    else:
        # Survival bonus: up to +10% for completing longer evaluations without crashing
        dur_excess = max(0.0, trial_dur - FITNESS_BASE_DURATION_S)
        dur_span = max(1.0, EVAL_DURATION_MAX_S - FITNESS_BASE_DURATION_S)
        bonus_factor = min(1.0, dur_excess / dur_span)
        duration_bonus_mult = 1.0 + (FITNESS_DURATION_BONUS_MAX * bonus_factor)
        return float(base_score * duration_bonus_mult)



=== FILE: 5_webots/optimization/ga_planner_optimizer/launch_ga_optimization.py ===
"""
================================================================================
  1-CLICK LAUNCHER FOR HEADLESS OPTIMIZER (C++ CURVILINEAR SL-MPC)
================================================================================
  Usage:
    python launch_ga_optimization.py
    python launch_ga_optimization.py --algorithm CMA_ES --generations 50 --cores 14
================================================================================
"""

import os
import sys
import argparse
import subprocess

CURR_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
GA_OPTIMIZER_DIR = CURR_DIR
WORKSPACE_ROOT = os.path.dirname(GA_OPTIMIZER_DIR)

for p in [GA_OPTIMIZER_DIR, WORKSPACE_ROOT]:
    if p not in sys.path and os.path.exists(p):
        sys.path.insert(0, p)

from ga_planner_config import (
    NUM_CPU_CORES,
    POPULATION_SIZE,
    NUM_GENERATIONS,
    EVALUATION_DURATION_S,
    ACTIVE_PLANNER,
    ALGORITHM,
    USE_TRACK2,
)


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Launch Headless Optimizer for Curvilinear SL-MPC")
    parser.add_argument("--planner", type=str, default=ACTIVE_PLANNER, help="Path planner to optimize (default: OPTION_2_SL_MPC)")
    parser.add_argument("--algorithm", type=str, default=ALGORITHM, choices=["CMA_ES", "cma_es", "cma", "GA", "ga", "genetic_algorithm"], help="Optimization engine (CMA_ES or GA)")
    parser.add_argument("--cores", type=int, default=NUM_CPU_CORES, help="Number of CPU cores / Webots instances")
    parser.add_argument("--pop-size", type=int, default=POPULATION_SIZE, help="Population size")
    parser.add_argument("--generations", type=int, default=NUM_GENERATIONS, help="Generations")
    parser.add_argument("--duration", type=float, default=EVALUATION_DURATION_S, help="Trial evaluation duration in sim seconds (default: 20s)")
    parser.add_argument("--resume", action="store_true", help="Resume from last checkpoint")
    parser.add_argument("--use-best-genome", "--use-best", "--seed-from-best", dest="use_best_genome", action="store_true", help="Seed optimizer using best_planner_genome.json instead of defaults")
    parser.add_argument("--seed-file", type=str, default=None, help="Custom path to genome JSON file to seed from")
    parser.add_argument("--no-track2", dest="use_track2", action="store_false", default=USE_TRACK2, help="Disable 2nd track (opposite 1.0 m/s obstacles) evaluations, evaluating only Track 1 (1.0 m/s)")
    parser.add_argument("--use-track2", dest="use_track2", action="store_true", help="Enable 2nd track (opposite 1.0 m/s obstacles) evaluations alongside Track 1 (1.0 m/s)")

    args = parser.parse_args()
    planner_key = "OPTION_2_SL_MPC"

    cmd = [
        sys.executable,
        os.path.join(GA_OPTIMIZER_DIR, "ga_optimizer.py"),
        "--planner", planner_key,
        "--algorithm", args.algorithm,
        "--cores", str(args.cores),
        "--pop-size", str(args.pop_size),
        "--generations", str(args.generations),
        "--duration", str(args.duration),
    ]
    if args.resume:
        cmd.append("--resume")
    if args.use_best_genome:
        cmd.append("--use-best-genome")
    if args.seed_file:
        cmd.extend(["--seed-file", args.seed_file])
    if not args.use_track2:
        cmd.append("--no-track2")
    else:
        cmd.append("--use-track2")

    print("=" * 80, flush=True)
    print("  LAUNCHING HEADLESS GA OPTIMIZER", flush=True)
    print(f"  Command: {' '.join(cmd)}", flush=True)
    print("=" * 80, flush=True)

    subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=GA_OPTIMIZER_DIR)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== FILE: 5_webots/optimization/ga_planner_optimizer/run_tight_10k_population.py ===
"""
================================================================================
  TARGETED SINGLE-GENERATION 84-POPULATION GA EVALUATOR AROUND 10.2k+ REGIME
================================================================================
  1. Loads known 10k+ champions (10.874k all-time champion, 10.259k CMA xbest).
  2. Constructs a population of 84 individuals tightly clustered around this regime:
     - Exact 10.874k champion and 10.259k CMA best
     - Convex interpolations between 10.8k and 10.2k
     - Micro-perturbations (1% - 3% span) around both peaks
  3. Evaluates all 84 candidates across 14 headless Webots workers in parallel.
  4. Records metrics, reports any candidates beating 10.2k and the champion, and
     updates the champion file if a new record is set.
================================================================================
"""

import os
import sys
import time
import json
import csv
import pickle
import numpy as np

CURR_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if CURR_DIR not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, CURR_DIR)

from ga_planner_config import (
    NUM_CPU_CORES,
    EVALUATION_DURATION_S,
    ACTIVE_PLANNER,
    Option2SLMPCGenome,
    OPTION_2_SL_MPC_GENE_SPECS,
)
from ga_evaluator import ParallelWorkerPool


def build_tight_population_84() -> list:
    """Builds 84 individuals clustered tightly around the >= 10.2k parameters."""
    # 1. Load 10.874k champion
    champ_path = os.path.join(CURR_DIR, "best_planner_genome.json")
    with open(champ_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        champ_json = json.load(f)
    champ_genes = champ_json["genes"]

    # 2. Load 10.259k CMA-ES best
    cma_path = os.path.join(CURR_DIR, "cma_state.pkl")
    with open(cma_path, "rb") as f:
        cma_data = pickle.load(f)
    es = cma_data["es"]

    g_dummy = Option2SLMPCGenome()
    lower, upper = g_dummy.get_bounds()
    span = upper - lower

    u_best = es.result.xbest
    x_cma = lower + u_best * span
    g_cma = Option2SLMPCGenome()
    g_cma.from_array(x_cma)
    cma_genes = g_cma.to_dict()

    pop = []

    # Candidate 0: Exact 10.874k Champion
    pop.append(Option2SLMPCGenome(champ_genes))
    # Candidate 1: Exact 10.259k CMA Best
    pop.append(Option2SLMPCGenome(cma_genes))

    arr_champ = Option2SLMPCGenome(champ_genes).to_array()
    arr_cma = Option2SLMPCGenome(cma_genes).to_array()

    # Candidates 2-15: Direct linear interpolations between 10.8k and 10.2k
    for alpha in np.linspace(0.05, 0.95, 14):
        interp = (1.0 - alpha) * arr_champ + alpha * arr_cma
        g = Option2SLMPCGenome()
        g.from_array(interp)
        pop.append(g)

    # Candidates 16-49: Ultra-tight Gaussian mutations around 10.8k Champion (sigma 0.5% - 2.5% span)
    np.random.seed(42)
    for sig in [0.005, 0.010, 0.015, 0.020, 0.025]:
        for _ in range(7):
            noise = np.random.normal(0.0, sig, size=span.shape) * span
            cand = np.clip(arr_champ + noise, lower, upper)
            g = Option2SLMPCGenome()
            g.from_array(cand)
            pop.append(g)

    # Candidates 50-83: Ultra-tight Gaussian mutations around 10.2k CMA Best (sigma 0.5% - 2.5% span)
    for sig in [0.005, 0.010, 0.015, 0.020, 0.025]:
        for _ in range(7):
            noise = np.random.normal(0.0, sig, size=span.shape) * span
            cand = np.clip(arr_cma + noise, lower, upper)
            g = Option2SLMPCGenome()
            g.from_array(cand)
            pop.append(g)

    # Truncate or pad to exactly 84
    while len(pop) < 84:
        noise = np.random.normal(0.0, 0.01, size=span.shape) * span
        cand = np.clip(arr_champ + noise, lower, upper)
        g = Option2SLMPCGenome()
        g.from_array(cand)
        pop.append(g)

    return pop[:84]


def main():
    print("=" * 80)
    print("  STARTING SINGLE-GENERATION 84-POPULATION GA EVALUATION")
    print(f"  Target: Clustered tightly around >= 10.2k parameter space")
    print(f"  Workers: {NUM_CPU_CORES} CPU Cores | Eval Duration: {EVALUATION_DURATION_S}s per candidate")
    print("=" * 80, flush=True)

    pop = build_tight_population_84()
    print(f"[Population] Constructed {len(pop)} candidates clustered around 10k+ peaks.\n", flush=True)

    pool = ParallelWorkerPool(num_workers=NUM_CPU_CORES)
    t0 = time.time()

    try:
        results = pool.evaluate_population(
            planner_name=ACTIVE_PLANNER,
            population_genomes=pop,
            duration_s=EVALUATION_DURATION_S,
        )
    finally:
        pool.shutdown()

    elapsed = time.time() - t0
    fitnesses = [r[0] for r in results]
    metrics_list = [r[1] for r in results]

    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print(f"  EVALUATION COMPLETE ({elapsed:.1f}s elapsed for 84 candidates)")
    print("=" * 80, flush=True)

    # Parse and sort results
    indexed_results = []
    for idx, (fit, met) in enumerate(results):
        indexed_results.append({
            "idx": idx,
            "fitness": fit,
            "metrics": met,
            "genome": pop[idx],
        })

    indexed_results.sort(key=lambda x: x["fitness"], reverse=True)

    # Count > 10k and > 10.2k
    count_10k = sum(1 for r in indexed_results if r["fitness"] >= 10000.0)
    count_10_2k = sum(1 for r in indexed_results if r["fitness"] >= 10200.0)

    print(f"\n[Summary Statistics]")
    print(f"  Total Evaluated:        {len(indexed_results)}")
    print(f"  Candidates >= 10,000:   {count_10k} / {len(indexed_results)} ({count_10k/len(indexed_results)*100:.1f}%)")
    print(f"  Candidates >= 10,200:   {count_10_2k} / {len(indexed_results)} ({count_10_2k/len(indexed_results)*100:.1f}%)")
    print(f"  Max Fitness Achieved:   {indexed_results[0]['fitness']:.2f}")
    print(f"  Mean Fitness:           {np.mean(fitnesses):.2f}")

    # Display all candidates with fitness >= 10,200
    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print("  ALL CANDIDATES WITH FITNESS >= 10,200:")
    print("=" * 80)
    print(f"{'Rank':<5} {'Cand#':<7} {'Fitness':<12} {'Dist (m)':<10} {'Speed (m/s)':<12} {'Laps':<6} {'Crashed'}")
    print("-" * 80)

    high_candidates = [r for r in indexed_results if r["fitness"] >= 10200.0]
    for rank, r in enumerate(high_candidates, 1):
        m = r["metrics"]
        print(f"{rank:<5} #{r['idx']:<6} {r['fitness']:<12.2f} {m.get('distance_m', 0.0):<10.2f} {m.get('mean_speed_mps', 0.0):<12.2f} {m.get('laps_completed', 0):<6} {m.get('crashed', False)}")

    # Check if we beat the all-time champion
    best_cand = indexed_results[0]
    champ_path = os.path.join(CURR_DIR, "best_planner_genome.json")
    with open(champ_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        curr_champ = json.load(f)

    curr_best_fit = curr_champ.get("fitness", 0.0)
    print("\n" + "-" * 80)
    print(f"Current Best Champion Fitness: {curr_best_fit:.2f}")
    print(f"Generation Top Candidate Fitness: {best_cand['fitness']:.2f}")

    if best_cand["fitness"] > curr_best_fit:
        print(f"  *** NEW ALL-TIME CHAMPION FOUND! Updating {champ_path} ***")
        with open(champ_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump({
                "planner": ACTIVE_PLANNER,
                "fitness": round(best_cand["fitness"], 2),
                "metrics": best_cand["metrics"],
                "genes": best_cand["genome"].to_dict(),
                "timestamp": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
            }, f, indent=2)
    else:
        print("  Existing champion remains unbeaten.")

    # Save detailed evaluation log to CSV
    data_dir = os.path.join(CURR_DIR, "data")
    os.makedirs(data_dir, exist_ok=True)
    csv_out = os.path.join(data_dir, "ga_tight_84_run_results.csv")
    with open(csv_out, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        writer = csv.writer(f)
        writer.writerow(["Rank", "Candidate_Idx", "Fitness", "Distance_m", "Mean_Speed_mps", "Laps", "Crashed", "Crash_Reason"])
        for rank, r in enumerate(indexed_results, 1):
            m = r["metrics"]
            writer.writerow([
                rank,
                r["idx"],
                round(r["fitness"], 2),
                round(m.get("distance_m", 0.0), 3),
                round(m.get("mean_speed_mps", 0.0), 3),
                m.get("laps_completed", 0),
                m.get("crashed", False),
                m.get("crash_reason", ""),
            ])
    print(f"\n[Artifact] Full results saved to: {csv_out}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== FILE: 5_webots/optimization/ga_planner_optimizer/run_tight_50m_regime.py ===
"""
================================================================================
  TARGETED 2-GENERATION TIGHT EXPLORATION (SIGMA = 0.05)
  - Gen 1: Explicitly includes the 32 historical >50m candidate genomes + 10 tight mutations (Pop=42)
  - Gen 2: Follow-up CMA-ES generation with tight sigma = 0.05 around top performers
================================================================================
"""

import os
import sys
import time
import json
import csv
import numpy as np

CURR_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if CURR_DIR not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, CURR_DIR)

from ga_planner_config import (
    NUM_CPU_CORES,
    POPULATION_SIZE,
    EVALUATION_DURATION_S,
    ACTIVE_PLANNER,
    Option2SLMPCGenome,
    load_best_planner_genome,
)
from ga_evaluator import ParallelWorkerPool
from ga_optimizer import CMAESOptimizerAdapter


def build_gen1_population(target_pop_size: int = 42) -> list:
    """Builds Gen 1 population with the 32 historical candidate genomes + tight mutations."""
    champ_genome, champ_fit, champ_metrics = load_best_planner_genome()
    lower, upper = champ_genome.get_bounds()
    span = upper - lower
    span[span <= 1e-9] = 1.0

    # Load checkpoint population
    ckpt_path = os.path.join(CURR_DIR, "ga_checkpoint.json")
    pop_dicts = []
    if os.path.exists(ckpt_path):
        with open(ckpt_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            ckpt = json.load(f)
            pop_dicts = ckpt.get("population", [])

    pop = []
    # Candidate 0: Champion
    pop.append(champ_genome)

    # Candidates 1..31: Distinct individuals from the checkpoint population
    for ind_dict in pop_dicts:
        if len(pop) >= 32:
            break
        # Avoid duplicate champion
        g = Option2SLMPCGenome(ind_dict)
        if len(pop) == 1 or np.max(np.abs(g.to_array() - champ_genome.to_array())) > 1e-4:
            pop.append(g)

    # If we need more up to 32, create tight micro-interpolations around champion
    arr_champ = champ_genome.to_array()
    np.random.seed(42)
    while len(pop) < 32:
        noise = np.random.normal(0.0, 0.02, size=span.shape) * span
        cand_arr = np.clip(arr_champ + noise, lower, upper)
        g = Option2SLMPCGenome()
        g.from_array(cand_arr)
        pop.append(g)

    # Pad remaining 10 candidates with tight sigma=0.05 mutations to reach pop_size=42
    while len(pop) < target_pop_size:
        noise = np.random.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=span.shape) * span
        cand_arr = np.clip(arr_champ + noise, lower, upper)
        g = Option2SLMPCGenome()
        g.from_array(cand_arr)
        pop.append(g)

    return pop[:target_pop_size]


def run_tight_exploration(num_generations: int = 2, sigma: float = 0.05, pop_size: int = 42):
    print("=" * 80)
    print("  TARGETED TIGHT 2-GENERATION GA EXPLORATION (SIGMA = 0.05)")
    print(f"  - Gen 1: Explicitly contains all 32 >50m historical genomes + tight variations")
    print(f"  - Gen 2: Follow-up CMA-ES tight exploration (sigma = {sigma:.3f})")
    print(f"  Workers: {NUM_CPU_CORES} Webots Instances | Eval Duration: {EVALUATION_DURATION_S}s")
    print("=" * 80, flush=True)

    champ_genome, champ_fit, champ_metrics = load_best_planner_genome()
    print(f"[Champion] Seed champion: Fitness = {champ_fit:.2f}, Distance = {champ_metrics.get('distance_m', 0.0):.2f}m\n", flush=True)

    gen1_pop = build_gen1_population(target_pop_size=pop_size)
    print(f"[Population] Gen 1 loaded with {len(gen1_pop)} candidates (including all 32 >50m candidate genomes).\n", flush=True)

    adapter = CMAESOptimizerAdapter(
        genome_cls=Option2SLMPCGenome,
        pop_size=pop_size,
        sigma0=sigma,
        initial_genome=champ_genome,
    )
    # Align adapter to Gen 1
    adapter.set_current_population(gen1_pop)

    pool = ParallelWorkerPool(num_workers=NUM_CPU_CORES)
    t_start = time.time()

    all_eval_results = []
    curr_best_fit = champ_fit
    curr_best_genome = champ_genome
    curr_best_metrics = champ_metrics

    try:
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        # GENERATION 1: Evaluate the 32 >50m candidates + variations
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        print(f"================================================================================")
        print(f"  GENERATION 1 / {num_generations}  [Evaluating 32 >50m Candidates + Variations]")
        print(f"================================================================================", flush=True)
        print(f"--- Evaluating {len(gen1_pop)} Candidates on {NUM_CPU_CORES} Parallel Workers ---", flush=True)

        t_gen_start = time.time()
        results_gen1 = pool.evaluate_population(
            planner_name=ACTIVE_PLANNER,
            population_genomes=gen1_pop,
            duration_s=EVALUATION_DURATION_S,
        )
        t_gen_elapsed = time.time() - t_gen_start

        fitnesses_gen1 = [r[0] for r in results_gen1]
        metrics_gen1 = [r[1] for r in results_gen1]

        best_idx1 = int(np.argmax(fitnesses_gen1))
        gen1_best_fit = float(fitnesses_gen1[best_idx1])
        gen1_mean_fit = float(np.mean(fitnesses_gen1))
        gen1_best_metrics = metrics_gen1[best_idx1]
        gen1_crashes = sum(1 for m in metrics_gen1 if m.get("crashed", False))

        print(
            f"[Gen 01 Results] Best Fit: {gen1_best_fit:7.1f} | Mean Fit: {gen1_mean_fit:7.1f} | "
            f"Dist: {gen1_best_metrics.get('distance_m', 0.0):5.2f}m | "
            f"Speed: {gen1_best_metrics.get('mean_speed_mps', 0.0):4.2f}m/s | "
            f"Laps: {gen1_best_metrics.get('laps_completed', 0)} | "
            f"Crashes: {gen1_crashes:2d}/{pop_size} | Time: {t_gen_elapsed:4.1f}s",
            flush=True,
        )

        for idx, (fit, met) in enumerate(results_gen1):
            all_eval_results.append({
                "generation": 1,
                "candidate_idx": idx,
                "fitness": fit,
                "metrics": met,
                "genome": gen1_pop[idx],
            })

        if gen1_best_fit > curr_best_fit:
            curr_best_fit = gen1_best_fit
            curr_best_genome = gen1_pop[best_idx1]
            curr_best_metrics = gen1_best_metrics
            champ_path = os.path.join(CURR_DIR, "best_planner_genome.json")
            print(f"  *** NEW ALL-TIME CHAMPION FOUND! Fitness: {curr_best_fit:.2f}, Dist: {curr_best_metrics.get('distance_m', 0.0):.2f}m ***", flush=True)
            with open(champ_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump({
                    "planner": ACTIVE_PLANNER,
                    "fitness": round(curr_best_fit, 2),
                    "metrics": curr_best_metrics,
                    "genes": curr_best_genome.to_dict(),
                    "timestamp": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                }, f, indent=2)

        # Update CMA-ES with Gen 1 results
        adapter.tell(fitnesses_gen1)

        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        # GENERATION 2: Tight CMA-ES exploration around best performers (Sigma = 0.05)
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        print(f"\n================================================================================")
        print(f"  GENERATION 2 / {num_generations}  [Tight CMA-ES Exploration (Sigma = {sigma:.3f})]")
        print(f"================================================================================", flush=True)

        gen2_pop = adapter.ask(inject_baseline=True)
        print(f"--- Evaluating {len(gen2_pop)} Candidates on {NUM_CPU_CORES} Parallel Workers ---", flush=True)

        t_gen_start = time.time()
        results_gen2 = pool.evaluate_population(
            planner_name=ACTIVE_PLANNER,
            population_genomes=gen2_pop,
            duration_s=EVALUATION_DURATION_S,
        )
        t_gen_elapsed = time.time() - t_gen_start

        fitnesses_gen2 = [r[0] for r in results_gen2]
        metrics_gen2 = [r[1] for r in results_gen2]

        best_idx2 = int(np.argmax(fitnesses_gen2))
        gen2_best_fit = float(fitnesses_gen2[best_idx2])
        gen2_mean_fit = float(np.mean(fitnesses_gen2))
        gen2_best_metrics = metrics_gen2[best_idx2]
        gen2_crashes = sum(1 for m in metrics_gen2 if m.get("crashed", False))

        print(
            f"[Gen 02 Results] Best Fit: {gen2_best_fit:7.1f} | Mean Fit: {gen2_mean_fit:7.1f} | "
            f"Dist: {gen2_best_metrics.get('distance_m', 0.0):5.2f}m | "
            f"Speed: {gen2_best_metrics.get('mean_speed_mps', 0.0):4.2f}m/s | "
            f"Laps: {gen2_best_metrics.get('laps_completed', 0)} | "
            f"Crashes: {gen2_crashes:2d}/{pop_size} | Time: {t_gen_elapsed:4.1f}s",
            flush=True,
        )

        for idx, (fit, met) in enumerate(results_gen2):
            all_eval_results.append({
                "generation": 2,
                "candidate_idx": idx,
                "fitness": fit,
                "metrics": met,
                "genome": gen2_pop[idx],
            })

        if gen2_best_fit > curr_best_fit:
            curr_best_fit = gen2_best_fit
            curr_best_genome = gen2_pop[best_idx2]
            curr_best_metrics = gen2_best_metrics
            champ_path = os.path.join(CURR_DIR, "best_planner_genome.json")
            print(f"  *** NEW ALL-TIME CHAMPION FOUND! Fitness: {curr_best_fit:.2f}, Dist: {curr_best_metrics.get('distance_m', 0.0):.2f}m ***", flush=True)
            with open(champ_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump({
                    "planner": ACTIVE_PLANNER,
                    "fitness": round(curr_best_fit, 2),
                    "metrics": curr_best_metrics,
                    "genes": curr_best_genome.to_dict(),
                    "timestamp": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                }, f, indent=2)

        adapter.tell(fitnesses_gen2)

    finally:
        pool.shutdown()

    total_elapsed = time.time() - t_start
    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print(f"  TIGHT EXPLORATION RUN COMPLETE ({total_elapsed:.1f}s total)")
    print("=" * 80, flush=True)

    # Summary of Top Candidates
    all_eval_results.sort(key=lambda x: x["fitness"], reverse=True)
    print("\n=== TOP 10 CANDIDATES FROM TIGHT EXPLORATION RUN ===")
    print(f"{'Rank':<5} {'Gen':<5} {'Cand#':<7} {'Fitness':<12} {'Dist (m)':<10} {'Speed (m/s)':<12} {'Laps':<6} {'Crashed'}")
    print("-" * 80)
    for rank, r in enumerate(all_eval_results[:10], 1):
        m = r["metrics"]
        print(f"{rank:<5} {r['generation']:<5} #{r['candidate_idx']:<6} {r['fitness']:<12.2f} {m.get('distance_m', 0.0):<10.2f} {m.get('mean_speed_mps', 0.0):<12.2f} {m.get('laps_completed', 0):<6} {m.get('crashed', False)}")

    # Save detailed CSV
    data_dir = os.path.join(CURR_DIR, "data")
    os.makedirs(data_dir, exist_ok=True)
    out_csv = os.path.join(data_dir, "tight_005_exploration_results.csv")
    with open(out_csv, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        writer = csv.writer(f)
        writer.writerow(["Rank", "Generation", "Candidate_Idx", "Fitness", "Distance_m", "Mean_Speed_mps", "Laps", "Crashed", "Crash_Reason"])
        for rank, r in enumerate(all_eval_results, 1):
            m = r["metrics"]
            writer.writerow([
                rank,
                r["generation"],
                r["candidate_idx"],
                round(r["fitness"], 2),
                round(m.get("distance_m", 0.0), 3),
                round(m.get("mean_speed_mps", 0.0), 3),
                m.get("laps_completed", 0),
                m.get("crashed", False),
                m.get("crash_reason", ""),
            ])
    print(f"\n[Artifact] Full results saved to: {out_csv}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_tight_exploration(num_generations=2, sigma=0.05, pop_size=42)


=== FILE: 5_webots/optimization/ga_planner_optimizer/test_ga_optimizer_offline.py ===
"""
Offline unit and validation tests for GA planner config, chromosome codecs, and reverse obstacle engine.
"""

import os
import sys
import numpy as np

CURR_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
GA_DIR = CURR_DIR
WEBOTS_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(GA_DIR))
MY_PROJECT_DIR = os.path.join(WEBOTS_DIR, "simulation", "my_project")
PLANNER_DIR = os.path.join(MY_PROJECT_DIR, "controllers", "tof_path_planner")
WORKSPACE_ROOT = os.path.dirname(WEBOTS_DIR)

for p in [GA_DIR, PLANNER_DIR, WORKSPACE_ROOT]:
    if p not in sys.path and os.path.exists(p):
        sys.path.insert(0, p)

from ga_planner_config import (
    Option2SLMPCGenome,
    get_genome_class,
    compute_scalar_fitness,
    OPTION_2_SL_MPC_GENE_SPECS,
    load_best_planner_genome,
    generate_logical_initial_population,
    BEST_GENOME_PATH,
)
from obstacle_engine import ReverseObstacleEngine


def test_genome_codecs():
    print("Testing Genome Codecs...")
    g2 = Option2SLMPCGenome()
    arr2 = g2.to_array()
    assert len(arr2) == len(OPTION_2_SL_MPC_GENE_SPECS)
    g2.from_array(arr2)
    g2_dict = g2.to_dict()
    assert "horizon_N" in g2_dict
    assert isinstance(g2_dict["horizon_N"], int)
    print(f"  -> Option 2 C++ SL-MPC: {len(arr2)} genes verified.")


def test_reverse_obstacle_engine():
    print("Testing Reverse Obstacle Engine...")
    csv_path = os.path.join(GA_DIR, "data", "cyan_champion_trajectory.csv")
    engine = ReverseObstacleEngine(csv_path, speed_mps=0.50)
    assert engine.total_length > 10.0
    print(f"  -> Track total length: {engine.total_length:.3f} m")

    p0 = engine.get_position_at_s(0.0)
    p5 = engine.get_position_at_s(5.0)
    p10 = engine.get_position_at_s(10.0)
    assert isinstance(p0, tuple) and len(p0) == 2
    assert isinstance(p5, tuple) and len(p5) == 2
    assert isinstance(p10, tuple) and len(p10) == 2
    print(f"  -> Pose at s=0m:  ({p0[0]:.3f}, {p0[1]:.3f})")
    print(f"  -> Pose at s=5m:  ({p5[0]:.3f}, {p5[1]:.3f})")
    print(f"  -> Pose at s=10m: ({p10[0]:.3f}, {p10[1]:.3f})")

    dt = 0.016
    n_obs = 3
    for step in range(5):
        sim_t = step * dt
        for k in range(n_obs):
            obs_s = (k * (engine.total_length / n_obs) - 0.50 * sim_t) % engine.total_length
            x, y = engine.get_position_at_s(obs_s)
            assert not np.isnan(x) and not np.isnan(y)

    # Verify start/finish line (s=0) continuity without teleportation jump
    p_pos = engine.get_position_at_s(0.001)
    p_neg = engine.get_position_at_s(-0.001)
    gap_across_start = np.hypot(p_pos[0] - p_neg[0], p_pos[1] - p_neg[1])
    assert gap_across_start < 0.005, f"Discontinuity at start line: {gap_across_start:.4f} m"
    print(f"  -> Start line s=0 continuity verified (gap for 2mm ds = {gap_across_start*1000:.3f} mm).")
    print("  -> Reverse progression test passed.")


def test_fitness_computation():
    print("Testing Fitness Function & Duration Scaling...")
    clean_metrics = {"distance_m": 22.5, "mean_speed_mps": 2.2, "laps_completed": 1, "crashed": False, "stalled": False}
    fit_clean = compute_scalar_fitness(clean_metrics)
    assert fit_clean > 2000.0
    print(f"  -> Clean run fitness (22.5m, 1 lap, default 25s): {fit_clean:.1f}")

    crash_metrics = {"distance_m": 5.0, "mean_speed_mps": 1.5, "laps_completed": 0, "crashed": True, "stalled": False}
    fit_crash = compute_scalar_fitness(crash_metrics)
    print(f"  -> Crashed run fitness (5.0m): {fit_crash:.1f}")
    assert fit_crash < fit_clean and fit_crash > 0.0

    # 1. Verify duration normalization: longer sim without crashing doesn't inflate ~2.4x, but gets ~10% bonus
    # Baseline 25s run at 2.5 m/s
    m_25s = {"distance_m": 62.5, "mean_speed_mps": 2.5, "laps_completed": 4, "crashed": False, "stalled": False, "eval_duration_s": 25.0}
    f_25s = compute_scalar_fitness(m_25s)

    # 60s run at identical speed (2.5 m/s) -> 2.4x distance and laps (150m, 10 laps)
    m_60s = {"distance_m": 150.0, "mean_speed_mps": 2.5, "laps_completed": 10, "crashed": False, "stalled": False, "eval_duration_s": 60.0}
    f_60s = compute_scalar_fitness(m_60s)

    # Without normalization f_60s would be ~24,000 (+140% inflation).
    # With normalization + 10% bonus: f_60s should be ~1.10x of f_25s!
    ratio = f_60s / f_25s
    print(f"  -> Duration Scaling: 25s clean = {f_25s:.1f}, 60s clean = {f_60s:.1f} (Ratio: {ratio:.3f}x, expected ~1.100x)")
    assert 1.09 <= ratio <= 1.11, f"Expected ~1.10x ratio, got {ratio:.4f}"

    # 50s run at identical speed -> 2.0x distance and laps (125m, 8 laps)
    m_50s = {"distance_m": 125.0, "mean_speed_mps": 2.5, "laps_completed": 8, "crashed": False, "stalled": False, "eval_duration_s": 50.0}
    f_50s = compute_scalar_fitness(m_50s)
    ratio_50 = f_50s / f_25s
    print(f"  -> Duration Scaling: 50s clean = {f_50s:.1f} (Ratio: {ratio_50:.3f}x, expected ~1.071x)")
    assert 1.06 <= ratio_50 <= 1.08, f"Expected ~1.071x ratio, got {ratio_50:.4f}"

    # 2. Verify crashed runs: no survival bonus awarded, normalized distance stays consistent
    m_crash_25s = {"distance_m": 31.25, "mean_speed_mps": 2.5, "laps_completed": 2, "crashed": True, "stalled": False, "eval_duration_s": 25.0}
    m_crash_60s = {"distance_m": 75.0, "mean_speed_mps": 2.5, "laps_completed": 5, "crashed": True, "stalled": False, "eval_duration_s": 60.0}
    f_crash_25s = compute_scalar_fitness(m_crash_25s)
    f_crash_60s = compute_scalar_fitness(m_crash_60s)
    diff_crash = abs(f_crash_60s - f_crash_25s)
    print(f"  -> Halfway crash: 25s trial = {f_crash_25s:.1f}, 60s trial = {f_crash_60s:.1f} (Difference: {diff_crash:.2f})")
    assert diff_crash <= 20.0, "Normalized crashed fitness across different durations should closely match within lap rounding!"



def test_load_best_genome_and_seeding():
    print("Testing Best Genome Loading & Initial Population Seeding...")
    assert os.path.exists(BEST_GENOME_PATH), f"File {BEST_GENOME_PATH} not found"
    loaded_g, fit, metrics = load_best_planner_genome(BEST_GENOME_PATH)
    assert loaded_g is not None, "Failed to load genome from best_planner_genome.json"
    assert fit > 0.0, f"Expected positive fitness, got {fit}"
    print(f"  -> Successfully loaded best genome: Fitness={fit:.2f}, Dist={metrics.get('distance_m', 0):.2f}m")

    # Generate population seeded from best genome
    pop = generate_logical_initial_population(Option2SLMPCGenome, pop_size=10, seed_genome=loaded_g)
    assert len(pop) == 10
    # Candidate 0 must match loaded genome
    loaded_dict = loaded_g.to_dict()
    cand0_dict = pop[0].to_dict()
    for k in ["q_progress", "q_cte", "r_steer", "v_corner_factor"]:
        assert math.isclose(cand0_dict[k], loaded_dict[k], rel_tol=1e-3), f"Mismatch in gene {k}: {cand0_dict[k]} vs {loaded_dict[k]}"
    print("  -> Verified Candidate 0 accurately seeded with best_planner_genome.json parameters.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import math
    test_genome_codecs()
    test_reverse_obstacle_engine()
    test_fitness_computation()
    test_load_best_genome_and_seeding()
    print("\nALL GA OPTIMIZER OFFLINE TESTS PASSED!")


=== FILE: 5_webots/robotics/ros2_cartographer/README.md ===
# ROS 2 & Google Cartographer SLAM (Experimental / Simulation Mapping)

> [!NOTE]
> **Hardware Context**: This module serves as an experimental simulation reference for 2D SLAM and offline track map generation. The physical Folkrace racing vehicle operates with **zero LiDAR**, relying exclusively on a solid-state quad VL53L8CX Time-of-Flight array and Curvilinear SL-MPC.

This package provides an integration between Webots simulation, ROS 2 Humble, and Google Cartographer 2D SLAM.

---

## Directory Structure

```
5_webots/robotics/ros2_cartographer/
├── config/
│   └── folkrace_2d.lua                  # Cartographer 2D configuration (20mm resolution, 6x6m arena)
├── docs/
│   └── 5_webots/robotics/ros2_cartographer_webots_guide.md# Complete installation, topic mappings & architecture guide
├── maps/
│   ├── folkrace_track_map.pgm           # 20mm Occupancy grid map generated from Track JSON
│   └── folkrace_track_map.yaml          # Nav2 Map Server metadata
├── scripts/
│   ├── install_webots/robotics/ros2_cartographer_wsl.sh # One-click setup script for ROS 2 & Cartographer in WSL2
│   ├── launch_cartographer.sh           # Cartographer SLAM launcher script
│   ├── webots_cartographer_bridge.py    # Python bridge streaming /scan, /imu & static TF
│   ├── convert_track_json_to_ros_map.py # JSON track layout to ROS Occupancy Grid generator
│   └── track_lidar_simulator.py         # Virtual LiDAR raycast engine
└── sim_validation/
    ├── run_solid_cartographer_test.py   # Vectorized SLAM engine with 100% opaque solid walls
    ├── run_fast_cartographer_test.py    # High-speed SLAM reconstructor
    └── run_cartographer_on_backup_champion.py # SLAM runner for backup champion tracks
```

---

## Quick Usage

### 1. Launch Cartographer in WSL2:
```bash
~/folkrace_cartographer_ws/launch_cartographer.sh
```

### 2. Stream LiDAR & IMU from Webots:
Run the bridge script or link `WebotsCartographerBridgeNode` directly in your Webots Python controller.

### 3. Generate a Map from any Track JSON:
```bash
python 5_webots/robotics/ros2_cartographer/scripts/convert_track_json_to_ros_map.py "<path_to_track.json>" "5_webots/robotics/ros2_cartographer/maps"
```

=== FILE: 5_webots/robotics/ros2_cartographer/config/folkrace_2d.lua ===
include "map_builder.lua"
include "trajectory_builder.lua"

-- =============================================================================
--  GOOGLE CARTOGRAPHER 2D SLAM TUNED FOR 6x6m HIGH-SPEED FOLKRACE CAR ON PI 5
--  Sensors: ms200k 360-LiDAR (450 pts) + BNO08X IMU (Gyro + Accel + Orientation)
-- =============================================================================

options = {
  map_builder = MAP_BUILDER,
  trajectory_builder = TRAJECTORY_BUILDER,
  map_frame = "map",
  tracking_frame = "imu_link",            -- Tracks IMU at Center of Gravity for high-freq rotation
  published_frame = "base_link",          -- Direct transform from map to base_link
  odom_frame = "odom",
  provide_odom_frame = false,
  publish_frame_projected_to_2d = true,
  publish_tracked_pose = true,            -- Publishes /tracked_pose for real-time MPPI vehicle localization
  use_odometry = false,                   -- Set true if high-precision wheel odometry is fed
  use_nav_sat = false,
  use_landmarks = false,
  num_laser_scans = 1,
  num_multi_echo_laser_scans = 0,
  num_subdivisions_per_laser_scan = 1,
  num_point_clouds = 0,
  lookup_transform_timeout_sec = 0.2,
  submap_publish_period_sec = 0.3,
  pose_publish_period_sec = 5e-3,         -- 200 Hz fast pose output for racing
  trajectory_publish_period_sec = 30e-3,
  rangefinder_sampling_ratio = 1.,
  odometry_sampling_ratio = 1.,
  fixed_frame_pose_sampling_ratio = 1.,
  imu_sampling_ratio = 1.,                -- 100% IMU packets processed for slip/yaw detection
  landmarks_sampling_ratio = 1.,
}

MAP_BUILDER.use_trajectory_builder_2d = true
MAP_BUILDER.num_background_threads = 4    -- Utilizes all 4 Cortex-A76 cores on Pi 5

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. LOCAL SLAM & SENSOR WEIGHT PREFERENCES (LiDAR vs IMU)
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.use_imu_data = true
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.min_range = 0.05
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.max_range = 4.50
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.min_z = -0.10
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.max_z = 0.50
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.missing_data_ray_length = 0.0

-- Sensor weighting: Prioritize sharp LiDAR scan matching with strong IMU yaw stabilization
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.ceres_scan_matcher.occupied_space_weight = 20.0   -- High LiDAR geometric trust
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.ceres_scan_matcher.translation_weight = 10.0      -- Translation trust
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.ceres_scan_matcher.rotation_weight = 40.0         -- Heavy rotational penalty to prevent scan yaw jump
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.imu_gravity_time_constant = 5.0

-- Real-time Correlative Scan Matcher (Ceres seed initializer for dynamic motion)
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.use_online_correlative_scan_matching = true
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.real_time_correlative_scan_matcher.linear_search_window = 0.10   -- 10 cm search window in 6x6m
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.real_time_correlative_scan_matcher.angular_search_window = math.rad(15.)
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.real_time_correlative_scan_matcher.translation_delta_cost_weight = 1e-1
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.real_time_correlative_scan_matcher.rotation_delta_cost_weight = 1e-1

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. HIT / MISS PROBABILITIES & DYNAMIC OBSTACLE DELETION (Grid Model)
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.submaps.grid_options_2d.grid_type = "PROBABILITY_GRID"
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.submaps.grid_options_2d.resolution = 0.02          -- 20 mm high-precision resolution

-- Probability Updates:
-- hit_probability: How quickly a solid wall/pillar is confirmed (0.55 -> ~2-3 hits to mark solid)
-- miss_probability: How aggressively empty laser rays clear out transient/ghost hits (0.47 = fast clearance)
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.submaps.range_data_inserter.probability_grid_range_data_inserter.hit_probability = 0.58
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.submaps.range_data_inserter.probability_grid_range_data_inserter.miss_probability = 0.46
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.submaps.range_data_inserter.probability_grid_range_data_inserter.insert_free_space = true

-- Submap size: 60 range insertions per submap (~15 submaps for entire 6x6 circuit)
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.submaps.num_range_data = 60

-- Motion filter: Inserts on motion (dx >= 4cm or dtheta >= 2 deg); does not spam submaps when stationary
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.motion_filter.max_time_seconds = 5.0
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.motion_filter.max_distance_meters = 0.04
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.motion_filter.max_angle_radians = math.rad(2.0)

-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. GLOBAL POSE GRAPH & LOOP CLOSURE OPTIMIZATION (Loop Closing on 6x6 Circuit)
-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
POSE_GRAPH.optimize_every_n_nodes = 25                                   -- Run loop closure every 25 submap nodes
POSE_GRAPH.constraint_builder.min_score = 0.60                           -- Score threshold to accept loop closures
POSE_GRAPH.constraint_builder.global_localization_min_score = 0.65
POSE_GRAPH.constraint_builder.max_constraint_distance = 6.0              -- 6m diagonal bounds for 6x6m arena
POSE_GRAPH.constraint_builder.fast_correlative_scan_matcher.linear_search_window = 3.0
POSE_GRAPH.constraint_builder.fast_correlative_scan_matcher.angular_search_window = math.rad(180.)

-- Pose Graph Optimization Weights
POSE_GRAPH.optimization_problem.huber_scale = 1e2
POSE_GRAPH.optimization_problem.acceleration_weight = 1e3
POSE_GRAPH.optimization_problem.rotation_weight = 3e5
POSE_GRAPH.optimization_problem.local_slam_pose_translation_weight = 1e5
POSE_GRAPH.optimization_problem.local_slam_pose_rotation_weight = 1e5
POSE_GRAPH.optimization_problem.odometry_translation_weight = 1e5
POSE_GRAPH.optimization_problem.odometry_rotation_weight = 1e5

return options

=== FILE: 5_webots/robotics/ros2_cartographer/docs/ros2_cartographer_webots_guide.md ===
# Comprehensive Setup Guide: ROS 2 + Google Cartographer + Webots LiDAR & IMU Integration

This guide provides the complete, end-to-end setup instructions to install ROS 2 and Google Cartographer in WSL2 (Ubuntu 22.04 LTS / Jammy or Ubuntu 24.04 LTS / Noble), build the ROS 2 Cartographer integration packages, and bridge Webots LiDAR and IMU telemetry to Cartographer for SLAM mapping and localization.

---

## 1. WSL 2 Ubuntu Installation & Initialization

From your Windows PowerShell terminal:

```powershell
# Install Ubuntu 22.04 LTS (Recommended for ROS 2 Humble) or Ubuntu 24.04 LTS (ROS 2 Jazzy)
wsl --install -d Ubuntu-22.04

# Launch Ubuntu in WSL2
wsl -d Ubuntu-22.04
```

---

## 2. ROS 2 Installation (Inside WSL2 Ubuntu)

Run the following commands inside your WSL2 terminal:

```bash
# Update package index and set locale
sudo apt update && sudo apt install -y locales curl gnupg lsb-release
sudo locale-gen en_US en_US.UTF-8
sudo update-locale LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8
export LANG=en_US.UTF-8

# Add ROS 2 GPG key and official repository
sudo curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ros/rosdistro/master/ros.key -o /usr/share/keyrings/ros-archive-keyring.gpg
echo "deb [arch=$(dpkg --print-architecture) signed-by=/usr/share/keyrings/ros-archive-keyring.gpg] http://packages.ros.org/ros2/ubuntu $(lsb_release -cs) main" | sudo tee /etc/apt/sources.list.d/ros2.list > /dev/null

# Update and install ROS 2 Desktop and dev tools
sudo apt update
# For Ubuntu 22.04: ROS 2 Humble | For Ubuntu 24.04: ROS 2 Jazzy
sudo apt install -y ros-humble-desktop python3-colcon-common-extensions python3-rosdep python3-vcstool

# Initialize rosdep
sudo rosdep init
rosdep update

# Add ROS environment setup to bashrc
echo "source /opt/ros/humble/setup.bash" >> ~/.bashrc
source /opt/ros/humble/setup.bash
```

---

## 3. Google Cartographer Installation

Install Google Cartographer and the ROS 2 Cartographer packages directly via apt:

```bash
sudo apt update
sudo apt install -y \
  ros-humble-cartographer \
  ros-humble-cartographer-ros \
  ros-humble-nav2-map-server \
  ros-humble-tf2-tools \
  ros-humble-robot-state-publisher
```

---

## 4. Webots to ROS 2 Cartographer Architecture

The Webots robot has:
- **LiDAR**: `ms200k_lidar` (360 FOV, 450 points, 10-60 Hz)
- **IMU**: `bno08x_imu` (Orientation/Quaternion) + `accelerometer` + `gyro`
- **Frame Tree**: `map` -> `odom` -> `base_link` -> `laser` & `imu_link`

### Communication Pipeline:
1. **Webots Python Controller Bridge**: Connects to Webots controller API, reads `ms200k_lidar`, `gyro`, `accelerometer`, and `bno08x_imu`, and publishes ROS 2 standard topics:
   - `/scan` (`sensor_msgs/msg/LaserScan`)
   - `/imu` (`sensor_msgs/msg/Imu`)
   - `/odom` (`nav_msgs/msg/Odometry` or via TF odometry)
   - `/tf` (`tf2_msgs/msg/TFMessage`) for `base_link` -> `laser` & `imu_link`
2. **Google Cartographer Node**: Subscribes to `/scan` and `/imu`, performs 2D submap scan matching and loop closure, and outputs `/map` and optimized poses.

---

## 5. Cartographer Configuration (`folkrace_2d.lua`)

Create your Cartographer configuration file inside your ROS 2 workspace:

```lua
include "map_builder.lua"
include "trajectory_builder.lua"

options = {
  map_builder = MAP_BUILDER,
  trajectory_builder = TRAJECTORY_BUILDER,
  map_frame = "map",
  tracking_frame = "imu_link",           -- IMU frame for high-frequency inertial tracking
  published_frame = "odom",              -- Cartographer publishes map -> odom transform
  odom_frame = "odom",
  provide_odom_frame = false,
  publish_frame_projected_to_2d = true,
  use_odometry = false,                  -- Can be enabled if wheel encoders are published
  use_nav_sat = false,
  use_landmarks = false,
  num_laser_scans = 1,
  num_multi_echo_laser_scans = 0,
  num_subdivisions_per_laser_scan = 1,
  num_point_clouds = 0,
  lookup_transform_timeout_sec = 0.2,
  submap_publish_period_sec = 0.3,
  pose_publish_period_sec = 5e-3,
  trajectory_publish_period_sec = 30e-3,
  rangefinder_sampling_ratio = 1.,
  odometry_sampling_ratio = 1.,
  fixed_frame_pose_sampling_ratio = 1.,
  imu_sampling_ratio = 1.,
  landmarks_sampling_ratio = 1.,
}

MAP_BUILDER.use_trajectory_builder_2d = true

-- Real-time 2D SLAM parameters tailored for fast RC folkrace car
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.min_range = 0.12
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.max_range = 5.00
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.missing_data_ray_length = 5.0
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.use_imu_data = true
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.use_online_correlative_scan_matching = true
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.real_time_correlative_scan_matcher.linear_search_window = 0.15
TRAJECTORY_BUILDER_2D.real_time_correlative_scan_matcher.angular_search_window = math.rad(20.)

POSE_GRAPH.optimization_problem.huber_scale = 1e2
POSE_GRAPH.optimize_every_n_nodes = 35
POSE_GRAPH.constraint_builder.min_score = 0.55

return options
```

## 6. Webots ROS 2 Publisher Node (`webots_webots/robotics/ros2_cartographer_bridge.py`)

A clean bridge running in ROS 2 / Webots Python controller:

```python
import rclpy
from rclpy.node import Node
from sensor_msgs.msg import LaserScan, Imu
from geometry_msgs.msg import TransformStamped
import tf2_ros
import math
import time

class WebotsCartographerBridge(Node):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__('webots_cartographer_bridge')
        
        self.scan_pub = self.create_publisher(LaserScan, '/scan', 10)
        self.imu_pub = self.create_publisher(Imu, '/imu', 50)
        self.tf_broadcaster = tf2_ros.StaticTransformBroadcaster(self)
        
        self.publish_static_transforms()
        self.get_logger().info("Webots ROS 2 Cartographer Bridge Initialized.")

    def publish_static_transforms(self):
        # Base Link -> Laser Transform
        t_laser = TransformStamped()
        t_laser.header.stamp = self.get_clock().now().to_msg()
        t_laser.header.frame_id = "base_link"
        t_laser.child_frame_id = "laser"
        t_laser.transform.translation.x = 0.0
        t_laser.transform.translation.y = 0.01
        t_laser.transform.translation.z = 0.0725
        t_laser.transform.rotation.w = 1.0

        # Base Link -> IMU Link Transform
        t_imu = TransformStamped()
        t_imu.header.stamp = self.get_clock().now().to_msg()
        t_imu.header.frame_id = "base_link"
        t_imu.child_frame_id = "imu_link"
        t_imu.transform.translation.x = 0.0
        t_imu.transform.translation.y = -0.01
        t_imu.transform.translation.z = 0.015
        t_imu.transform.rotation.w = 1.0

        self.tf_broadcaster.sendTransform([t_laser, t_imu])

    def publish_lidar_scan(self, range_image, fov_rad, min_range, max_range, stamp_sec):
        scan_msg = LaserScan()
        scan_msg.header.stamp.sec = int(stamp_sec)
        scan_msg.header.stamp.nanosec = int((stamp_sec - int(stamp_sec)) * 1e9)
        scan_msg.header.frame_id = "laser"
        
        num_readings = len(range_image)
        scan_msg.angle_min = -fov_rad / 2.0
        scan_msg.angle_max = fov_rad / 2.0
        scan_msg.angle_increment = fov_rad / num_readings
        scan_msg.time_increment = 0.0
        scan_msg.scan_time = 0.02
        scan_msg.range_min = float(min_range)
        scan_msg.range_max = float(max_range)
        scan_msg.ranges = [float(r) for r in range_image]
        
        self.scan_pub.publish(scan_msg)

    def publish_imu(self, accel_xyz, gyro_xyz, quat_xyzw, stamp_sec):
        imu_msg = Imu()
        imu_msg.header.stamp.sec = int(stamp_sec)
        imu_msg.header.stamp.nanosec = int((stamp_sec - int(stamp_sec)) * 1e9)
        imu_msg.header.frame_id = "imu_link"
        
        imu_msg.orientation.x = float(quat_xyzw[0])
        imu_msg.orientation.y = float(quat_xyzw[1])
        imu_msg.orientation.z = float(quat_xyzw[2])
        imu_msg.orientation.w = float(quat_xyzw[3])
        
        imu_msg.angular_velocity.x = float(gyro_xyz[0])
        imu_msg.angular_velocity.y = float(gyro_xyz[1])
        imu_msg.angular_velocity.z = float(gyro_xyz[2])
        
        imu_msg.linear_acceleration.x = float(accel_xyz[0])
        imu_msg.linear_acceleration.y = float(accel_xyz[1])
        imu_msg.linear_acceleration.z = float(accel_xyz[2])
        
        self.imu_pub.publish(imu_msg)
```

## 7. Running SLAM with Cartographer

1. **Launch Cartographer Node**:
   ```bash
   ros2 launch cartographer_ros cartographer.launch.py \
     cartographer_config_dir:=/path/to/config \
     configuration_basename:=folkrace_2d.lua
   ```

2. **Launch Occupancy Grid Node (for standard `/map` visualization in RViz2)**:
   ```bash
   ros2 launch cartographer_ros occupancy_grid.launch.py \
     resolution:=0.05 \
     publish_period_sec:=1.0
   ```

3. **Launch RViz2**:
   ```bash
   rviz2
   ```
   - Set Fixed Frame to `map`.
   - Add **Map** display (`/map`).
   - Add **LaserScan** display (`/scan`).
   - Add **TF** display.


=== FILE: 5_webots/robotics/ros2_cartographer/scripts/check_map_empty.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
================================================================================
  MAP CONTENT VALIDATOR & NON-EMPTY CHECKER
  Verifies whether an exported PNG map contains rendered track data (non-empty).
================================================================================
"""

import sys
import os
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image

def check_map_file(png_path):
    if not os.path.exists(png_path):
        return False, "File does not exist"
    
    if os.path.getsize(png_path) < 1000:
        return False, "File size too small (<1KB)"

    try:
        im = Image.open(png_path).convert('RGB')
        arr = np.array(im)
        # Check standard deviation across channels/pixels to detect blank/solid frames
        std_dev = np.std(arr)
        unique_colors = len(np.unique(arr.reshape(-1, arr.shape[2]), axis=0))
        
        if std_dev < 1.0 or unique_colors < 10:
            return False, f"Blank image (std_dev={std_dev:.2f}, unique_colors={unique_colors})"
        
        return True, f"Valid map image (unique_colors={unique_colors}, std_dev={std_dev:.2f})"
    except Exception as e:
        return False, f"Image read error: {e}"

if __name__ == '__main__':
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print("Usage: python check_map_empty.py <path_to_png>")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    is_valid, msg = check_map_file(sys.argv[1])
    print(f"[{'VALID' if is_valid else 'EMPTY'}] {sys.argv[1]}: {msg}")
    sys.exit(0 if is_valid else 1)


=== FILE: 5_webots/robotics/ros2_cartographer/scripts/convert_track_json_to_ros_map.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Converts track_layout JSON into:
1. Standard ROS Occupancy Grid (.pgm image + .yaml metadata) for Nav2 Map Server
2. Accurate 2D Wall coordinates for verification and pure localization
"""

import os
import sys
import json
import math
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

DIR_VECTORS = [
    np.array([1.0, 0.0]),   # 0: East (+X)
    np.array([0.0, 1.0]),   # 1: North (+Y)
    np.array([-1.0, 0.0]),  # 2: West (-X)
    np.array([0.0, -1.0])   # 3: South (-Y)
]

LEFT_VECTORS = [
    np.array([0.0, 1.0]),   # 0: North
    np.array([-1.0, 0.0]),  # 1: West
    np.array([0.0, -1.0]),  # 2: South
    np.array([1.0, 0.0])    # 3: East
]

def build_polygons(track_data, step_size=0.01):
    params = track_data.get("parameters", {})
    tile_size = float(params.get("tileSize", 1.2))
    track_width = float(params.get("trackWidth", 1.0))
    blocks = track_data.get("blocks", [])
    
    half_width = track_width / 2.0
    left_wall, right_wall, centerline = [], [], []
    
    for b in blocks:
        b_type = b.get("type", "straight")
        start_x = float(b.get("startX", 0.0))
        start_y = float(b.get("startY", 0.0))
        cur_h = int(b.get("startHeading", 0)) % 4
        fwd = DIR_VECTORS[cur_h]
        left = LEFT_VECTORS[cur_h]
        
        if b_type == "straight":
            steps = max(10, int(math.ceil(tile_size / step_size)))
            for i in range(1, steps + 1):
                t = i / steps
                px = start_x + fwd[0] * tile_size * t
                py = start_y + fwd[1] * tile_size * t
                left_wall.append((px + left[0] * half_width, py + left[1] * half_width))
                right_wall.append((px - left[0] * half_width, py - left[1] * half_width))
                centerline.append((px, py))
                
        elif b_type == "turn_left":
            r_center = tile_size / 2.0
            c_data = b.get("center", {})
            cx = float(c_data.get("x", start_x + left[0] * r_center))
            cy = float(c_data.get("y", start_y + left[1] * r_center))
            start_angle = math.atan2(start_y - cy, start_x - cx)
            arc_len = (math.pi / 2.0) * r_center
            steps = max(20, int(math.ceil(arc_len / step_size)))
            
            for i in range(1, steps + 1):
                angle = start_angle + (math.pi / 2.0) * (i / steps)
                cos_a, sin_a = math.cos(angle), math.sin(angle)
                left_wall.append((cx + (r_center - half_width) * cos_a, cy + (r_center - half_width) * sin_a))
                right_wall.append((cx + (r_center + half_width) * cos_a, cy + (r_center + half_width) * sin_a))
                centerline.append((cx + r_center * cos_a, cy + r_center * sin_a))
                
        elif b_type == "turn_right":
            r_center = tile_size / 2.0
            c_data = b.get("center", {})
            cx = float(c_data.get("x", start_x - left[0] * r_center))
            cy = float(c_data.get("y", start_y - left[1] * r_center))
            start_angle = math.atan2(start_y - cy, start_x - cx)
            arc_len = (math.pi / 2.0) * r_center
            steps = max(20, int(math.ceil(arc_len / step_size)))
            
            for i in range(1, steps + 1):
                angle = start_angle - (math.pi / 2.0) * (i / steps)
                cos_a, sin_a = math.cos(angle), math.sin(angle)
                left_wall.append((cx + (r_center + half_width) * cos_a, cy + (r_center + half_width) * sin_a))
                right_wall.append((cx + (r_center - half_width) * cos_a, cy + (r_center - half_width) * sin_a))
                centerline.append((cx + r_center * cos_a, cy + r_center * sin_a))
                
    return left_wall, right_wall, centerline

def generate_occupancy_grid(json_path, output_dir, resolution=0.02, margin=0.5):
    with open(json_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        track_data = json.load(f)
        
    left_wall, right_wall, centerline = build_polygons(track_data)
    
    all_pts = left_wall + right_wall
    xs = [p[0] for p in all_pts]
    ys = [p[1] for p in all_pts]
    
    min_x = math.floor((min(xs) - margin) / resolution) * resolution
    max_x = math.ceil((max(xs) + margin) / resolution) * resolution
    min_y = math.floor((min(ys) - margin) / resolution) * resolution
    max_y = math.ceil((max(ys) + margin) / resolution) * resolution
    
    width_px = int(round((max_x - min_x) / resolution))
    height_px = int(round((max_y - min_y) / resolution))
    
    print(f"[MAP] Bounding box: X=[{min_x:.2f}, {max_x:.2f}], Y=[{min_y:.2f}, {max_y:.2f}]")
    print(f"[MAP] Image dimensions: {width_px} x {height_px} px at {resolution*1000:.1f}mm/px")
    
    # In ROS Occupancy Grid standard: 254 (0xFF) = Free, 0 (0x00) = Occupied (Wall), 205 = Unknown
    img = Image.new("L", (width_px, height_px), 205)
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
    
    def world_to_px(wx, wy):
        px = int(round((wx - min_x) / resolution))
        # Note: image origin is top-left, ROS origin is bottom-left
        py = height_px - 1 - int(round((wy - min_y) / resolution))
        return (px, py)
    
    # 1. Fill corridor / free space with 254 (White = Free)
    corridor_poly = [world_to_px(p[0], p[1]) for p in left_wall] + [world_to_px(p[0], p[1]) for p in reversed(right_wall)]
    draw.polygon(corridor_poly, fill=254)
    
    # 2. Draw Walls as Occupied (Black = 0) with thickness = 18mm
    wall_px_width = max(1, int(round(0.018 / resolution)))
    for poly in [left_wall, right_wall]:
        px_coords = [world_to_px(p[0], p[1]) for p in poly]
        draw.line(px_coords, fill=0, width=wall_px_width)
        
    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
    pgm_path = os.path.join(output_dir, "folkrace_track_map.pgm")
    yaml_path = os.path.join(output_dir, "folkrace_track_map.yaml")
    
    img.save(pgm_path)
    print(f"[MAP] Saved PGM map: {pgm_path}")
    
    yaml_content = f"""image: folkrace_track_map.pgm
mode: trinary
resolution: {resolution}
origin: [{min_x:.4f}, {min_y:.4f}, 0.000000]
negate: 0
occupied_thresh: 0.65
free_thresh: 0.25
"""
    with open(yaml_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(yaml_content)
    print(f"[MAP] Saved YAML metadata: {yaml_path}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    src_json = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else r"C:\Users\nurmi\Downloads\track_layout_1787771901940.json"
    out_dir = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else r"c:\Users\nurmi\Documents\Folkrace\maps"
    generate_occupancy_grid(src_json, out_dir, resolution=0.02)


=== FILE: 5_webots/robotics/ros2_cartographer/scripts/export_cartographer_png.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import sys
import os
import math
import rclpy
from rclpy.node import Node
from nav_msgs.msg import OccupancyGrid
from PIL import Image
import numpy as np

class MapSaverPNG(Node):
    def __init__(self, out_path):
        super().__init__('map_saver_png')
        self.out_path = out_path
        self.sub = self.create_subscription(OccupancyGrid, '/map', self.map_callback, 10)
        self.done = False

    def map_callback(self, msg):
        w = msg.info.width
        h = msg.info.height
        res = msg.info.resolution
        origin_x = msg.info.origin.position.x
        origin_y = msg.info.origin.position.y

        # Reject uninitialized or singular 0x0 grids from initial ROS2 topic creation
        if w <= 0 or h <= 0 or abs(origin_x) > 1e10 or abs(origin_y) > 1e10 or len(msg.data) == 0:
            self.get_logger().warn(f"Received uninitialized /map ({w}x{h}, origin=[{origin_x:.2f}, {origin_y:.2f}]), waiting for valid submaps...")
            return

        self.get_logger().info(f"Received valid /map from Cartographer: {w}x{h} cells (res={res:.3f}m, origin=[{origin_x:.2f}, {origin_y:.2f}])")
        
        # Grid data: -1=Unknown, 0=Free, 100=Occupied
        raw_data = np.array(msg.data, dtype=np.int8).reshape((h, w))
        
        # Color mapping: Occupied -> Yellow/White, Free -> Dark, Unknown -> Mid-gray
        # Matplotlib visualization
        try:
            import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 8), dpi=200, facecolor='#0d1117')
            ax.set_facecolor('#0d1117')
            
            extent = [origin_x, origin_x + w * res, origin_y, origin_y + h * res]
            display_grid = np.zeros((h, w), dtype=np.float32)
            display_grid[raw_data == -1] = 0.5   # Unknown
            display_grid[raw_data == 0] = 0.0    # Free
            display_grid[raw_data > 50] = 1.0    # Occupied
            
            im = ax.imshow(display_grid, origin='lower', extent=extent, cmap='viridis', vmin=0.0, vmax=1.0)
            cbar = plt.colorbar(im, ax=ax, fraction=0.035, pad=0.04)
            cbar.set_label('Live Google Cartographer SLAM Occupancy', color='#c9d1d9', fontsize=9)
            cbar.ax.yaxis.set_tick_params(color='#8b949e')
            plt.setp(plt.getp(cbar.ax.axes, 'yticklabels'), color='#8b949e', fontsize=8)
            
            ax.set_title("Google Cartographer 2D SLAM - Live Webots Sensor Feed", color='white', fontsize=12, pad=12, fontweight='bold')
            ax.set_xlabel("X Position (meters)", color='#c9d1d9', fontsize=10)
            ax.set_ylabel("Y Position (meters)", color='#c9d1d9', fontsize=10)
            ax.tick_params(colors='#8b949e', labelsize=8)
            
            for spine in ax.spines.values():
                spine.set_color('#30363d')
                
            plt.tight_layout()
            plt.savefig(self.out_path, dpi=200)
            plt.close()
            self.get_logger().info(f"Saved pure Cartographer live map image to {self.out_path}")

            # Export standard ROS2 Cartographer PGM and YAML map files
            base_dir = os.path.dirname(self.out_path)
            # PGM encoding: 0 (black)=Occupied Wall, 254 (white)=Free, 205 (gray)=Unknown
            pgm_grid = np.full((h, w), 205, dtype=np.uint8)
            pgm_grid[raw_data == 0] = 254
            pgm_grid[raw_data > 50] = 0
            pgm_grid = np.flipud(pgm_grid)

            # Save in sweep results directory
            pgm_path = os.path.join(base_dir, "cartographer_2d_slam_static_map.pgm")
            with open(pgm_path, "wb") as f_pgm:
                f_pgm.write(f"P5\n{w} {h}\n255\n".encode("ascii"))
                f_pgm.write(pgm_grid.tobytes())

            yaml_path = os.path.join(base_dir, "cartographer_2d_slam_static_map.yaml")
            yaml_content = f"""image: cartographer_2d_slam_static_map.pgm
mode: trinary
resolution: {res}
origin: [{origin_x:.4f}, {origin_y:.4f}, 0.000000]
negate: 0
occupied_thresh: 0.65
free_thresh: 0.25
"""
            with open(yaml_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f_yaml:
                f_yaml.write(yaml_content)

            # Also update canonical map in maps/cyan_champion_clean_static/
            clean_dir = "/mnt/c/Users/nurmi/Documents/Folkrace/maps/cyan_champion_clean_static"
            try:
                os.makedirs(clean_dir, exist_ok=True)
                with open(os.path.join(clean_dir, "cartographer_2d_slam_static_map.pgm"), "wb") as f_clean:
                    f_clean.write(f"P5\n{w} {h}\n255\n".encode("ascii"))
                    f_clean.write(pgm_grid.tobytes())
                with open(os.path.join(clean_dir, "cartographer_2d_slam_static_map.yaml"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f_clean_yaml:
                    f_clean_yaml.write(yaml_content)
                self.get_logger().info(f"Updated canonical Cartographer SLAM map in {clean_dir}")
            except Exception as ex:
                self.get_logger().warn(f"Could not copy to canonical dir: {ex}")

            self.done = True
        except Exception as e:
            self.get_logger().error(f"Error rendering map PNG: {e}")

def main():
    rclpy.init()
    out_path = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "/mnt/c/Users/nurmi/Documents/Folkrace/maps/cyan_champion_clean_static/cartographer_2d_slam_static_result.png"
    node = MapSaverPNG(out_path)
    
    print("[MAP RENDERER] Waiting for live /map from Cartographer (up to 25s)...")
    for _ in range(250):
        rclpy.spin_once(node, timeout_sec=0.1)
        if node.done:
            break
            
    node.destroy_node()
    rclpy.shutdown()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()


=== FILE: 5_webots/robotics/ros2_cartographer/scripts/install_ros2_cartographer_wsl.sh ===
#!/bin/bash
set -e

echo "=== 1. Setting up locales ==="
apt-get update
apt-get install -y locales curl gnupg lsb-release
locale-gen en_US en_US.UTF-8
update-locale LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8
export LANG=en_US.UTF-8

echo "=== 2. Adding ROS 2 repository ==="
curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ros/rosdistro/master/ros.key -o /usr/share/keyrings/ros-archive-keyring.gpg
echo "deb [arch=$(dpkg --print-architecture) signed-by=/usr/share/keyrings/ros-archive-keyring.gpg] http://packages.ros.org/ros2/ubuntu $(lsb_release -cs) main" > /etc/apt/sources.list.d/ros2.list

echo "=== 3. Installing ROS 2 Humble Desktop & Cartographer ==="
apt-get update
DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive apt-get install -y \
  ros-humble-desktop \
  ros-humble-cartographer \
  ros-humble-cartographer-ros \
  ros-humble-nav2-map-server \
  ros-humble-tf2-tools \
  python3-colcon-common-extensions \
  python3-rosdep \
  python3-vcstool

echo "=== 4. Setting up Environment for user gold ==="
if [ ! -f /etc/ros/rosdep/sources.list.d/20-default.list ]; then
  rosdep init || true
fi
su - gold -c "rosdep update || true"

if ! grep -q "/opt/ros/humble/setup.bash" /home/gold/.bashrc; then
  echo "source /opt/ros/humble/setup.bash" >> /home/gold/.bashrc
fi

echo "=== Installation Completed Successfully ==="


=== FILE: 5_webots/robotics/ros2_cartographer/scripts/launch_cartographer.sh ===
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# ==============================================================================
# Launch Google Cartographer SLAM Node with Webots live UDP Bridge
# ==============================================================================
source /opt/ros/humble/setup.bash

CONFIG_DIR="${HOME}/folkrace_cartographer_ws/config"
SCRIPTS_DIR="${HOME}/folkrace_cartographer_ws/scripts"

echo "=============================================================================="
echo "  STARTING GOOGLE CARTOGRAPHER 2D SLAM (ROS 2 HUMBLE) + WEBOTS LIVE BRIDGE"
echo "=============================================================================="

# 1. Start Webots Live UDP -> ROS 2 Bridge in background
python3 "${SCRIPTS_DIR}/webots_cartographer_bridge.py" &
BRIDGE_PID=$!
echo "[LAUNCH] Webots UDP Bridge started (PID: ${BRIDGE_PID})."

# 2. Start Cartographer Node
ros2 run cartographer_ros cartographer_node \
    -configuration_directory "${CONFIG_DIR}" \
    -configuration_basename folkrace_2d.lua \
    --ros-args \
    --remap scan:=/scan \
    --remap imu:=/imu &
CARTO_PID=$!
echo "[LAUNCH] Cartographer Node started (PID: ${CARTO_PID})."

# 3. Start Cartographer Occupancy Grid Node
ros2 run cartographer_ros cartographer_occupancy_grid_node \
    -resolution 0.02 \
    -publish_period_sec 0.5 &
GRID_PID=$!
echo "[LAUNCH] Occupancy Grid Node started (PID: ${GRID_PID})."

echo "[LAUNCH] All Cartographer nodes running. Press Ctrl+C to stop."
wait


=== FILE: 5_webots/robotics/ros2_cartographer/scripts/save_live_map.sh ===
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# ==============================================================================
# Save Live Cartographer SLAM Map to PGM and YAML
# ==============================================================================
source /opt/ros/humble/setup.bash

OUT_DIR="${1:-${HOME}/folkrace_cartographer_ws/maps}"
